```text
    1  #include "CloudFolderValidation.h"
    2  #include <rapidjson/document.h>
    3  #include <set>
    4  
    5  namespace
    6  {
    7  const std::set<std::string> categories = {"saves", "settings", "roms", "bios", "media"};
    8  const std::set<std::string> states = {"present", "missing", "empty", "misplaced", "unreadable"};
    9  bool object(const rapidjson::Value& value)
   10  {
   11  	if (!value.IsObject()) return false;
   12  	std::set<std::string> names;
   13  	for (auto it = value.MemberBegin(); it != value.MemberEnd(); ++it)
   14  		if (!names.insert(std::string(it->name.GetString(), it->name.GetStringLength())).second)
   15  			return false;
   16  	return true;
   17  }
   18  bool string(const rapidjson::Value& value, const char* name, std::string& out)
   19  {
   20  	if (!value.HasMember(name) || !value[name].IsString()) return false;
   21  	out.assign(value[name].GetString(), value[name].GetStringLength());
   22  	// Paths and IDs go to single facts and commands, never terminal control text.
   23  	for (unsigned char c : out)
   24  		if (c < 32 || c == 127) return false;
   25  	return true;
   26  }
   27  bool document(const std::string& json, rapidjson::Document& doc)
   28  {
   29  	if (json.empty() || json.size() > 65536 || json.find('\0') != std::string::npos) return false;
   30  	doc.Parse(json.c_str());
   31  	return !doc.HasParseError() && object(doc) && doc.HasMember("schema_version")
   32  		&& doc["schema_version"].IsInt() && doc["schema_version"].GetInt() == 1;
   33  }
   34  }
   35  
   36  bool CloudFolderValidation::appendOutput(std::string& json, const char* data, std::size_t size)
   37  {
   38  	if (size > 65536 || json.size() > 65536 - size)
   39  	{
   40  		json.clear();
   41  		return false;
   42  	}
   43  	json.append(data, size);
   44  	return true;
   45  }
   46  
   47  CloudFolderValidation::Context CloudFolderValidation::parseContext(const std::string& json)
   48  {
   49  	Context c;
   50  	rapidjson::Document doc;
   51  	if (!document(json, doc) || !string(doc, "config_id", c.configId) || c.configId.empty()
   52  		|| !doc.HasMember("paths") || !object(doc["paths"])) return {};
   53  	for (const auto& key : categories)
   54  	{
   55  		std::string path;
   56  		if (!string(doc["paths"], key.c_str(), path) || path.empty()) return {};
   57  		c.paths[key] = path;
   58  	}
   59  	c.valid = true;
   60  	return c;
   61  }
   62  
   63  CloudFolderValidation::Result CloudFolderValidation::parseResult(const std::string& json,
   64  	const std::string& runId, const Context& started, const Context& current,
   65  	const std::vector<std::string>& selected)
   66  {
   67  	Result result;
   68  	if (runId.empty() || !started.valid || !current.valid || started.configId != current.configId
   69  		|| started.paths != current.paths || selected.empty()) return result;
   70  	std::set<std::string> expected;
   71  	for (const auto& key : selected)
   72  		if (!categories.count(key) || !expected.insert(key).second) return result;
   73  	rapidjson::Document doc;
   74  	std::string gotRun, gotConfig;
   75  	if (!document(json, doc) || !string(doc, "run_id", gotRun) || gotRun != runId
   76  		|| !string(doc, "config_id", gotConfig) || gotConfig != started.configId
   77  		|| !doc.HasMember("complete") || !doc["complete"].IsBool()
   78  		|| !doc.HasMember("categories") || !doc["categories"].IsArray()) return result;
   79  	std::set<std::string> seen;
   80  	for (const auto& row : doc["categories"].GetArray())
   81  	{
   82  		Category c;
   83  		if (!object(row) || !string(row, "category", c.category) || !expected.count(c.category)
   84  			|| !seen.insert(c.category).second || !string(row, "state", c.state) || !states.count(c.state)
   85  			|| !string(row, "path", c.path) || c.path != started.paths.at(c.category)
   86  			|| !string(row, "detail", c.detail)) return {};
   87  		result.categories.push_back(c);
   88  	}
   89  	if (seen != expected) return {};
   90  	result.complete = doc["complete"].GetBool();
   91  	// An unreadable category is never a completed check, regardless of the flag.
   92  	for (const auto& c : result.categories)
   93  		if (c.state == "unreadable") result.complete = false;
   94  	result.valid = true;
   95  	return result;
   96  }
```

```text
    9  // The identity and paths must still be those selected when the check started.
   10  namespace CloudFolderValidation

   15  	std::string configId;
   16  	std::map<std::string, std::string> paths;
   17  };
   18  struct Category
   19  {
   20  	std::string category, state, path, detail;
   21  };
   22  struct Result
   23  {
   24  	bool valid = false;
   25  	bool complete = false;
   26  	std::vector<Category> categories;
   27  };
   28  // Append raw pipe bytes without inventing line breaks; reject oversize output.
   29  bool appendOutput(std::string& json, const char* data, std::size_t size);
   30  Context parseContext(const std::string& json);
   31  Result parseResult(const std::string& json, const std::string& runId,
   32  	const Context& started, const Context& current, const std::vector<std::string>& selected);
   33  }
```

```text
   10  #include "LocaleES.h"

   12  namespace CloudOffer
   13  {
   14  
   15  // The cloud answered, and there is no saves folder in it: not a failure,
   16  // but it leaves the player with nothing restored and no obvious way
   17  // forward (#100, D-CLOUD-085).
   18  //
   19  // When the parent holds a folder whose name is within a typo of the one we
   20  // were told to read, the question changes (#127, D-CLOUD-091/092): creating
   21  // it would put /ROCKNIX/Savez beside the real /ROCKNIX/Saves, after which
   22  // every backup goes to the wrong folder and every restore finds nothing.
   23  // So the first choice offered is to fix the name, and creating it anyway is
   24  // the second.
   25  static void createSavesFolder(Window* window, const std::vector<std::string>& args)
   26  {
   27  	// What the script found: the folder it was told to read, and -- when
   28  	// there is one -- the folder beside it whose name is close to it.
   29  	const std::string folder = args.size() > 0 ? args[0] : "";
   30  	const std::string near = args.size() > 1 ? args[1] : "";
   31  
   32  	auto create = [window]
   33  	{
   34  		window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<int>(window, _("SETTING UP YOUR CLOUD FOLDERS"),
   35  			[](auto gui)
   36  			{
   37  				return ApiSystem::executeScriptLegacy("timeout 90 /usr/bin/cloud_setup --seed-folders saves",
   38  					[](const std::string) {}).second;
   39  			},
   40  			[window](int rc)
   41  			{
   42  				window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, rc == 0
   43  					? _("DONE. YOUR SAVES WILL GO THERE THE NEXT TIME YOU BACK THEM UP.")
   44  					: _("COULDN'T CREATE IT. CHECK YOUR CONNECTION AND TRY AGAIN FROM MANAGE CLOUD STORAGE."),
   45  					_("OK")));
   46  			}));
   47  	};
   48  
   49  	if (!near.empty())
   50  	{
   51  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
   52  			Utils::String::format(_("NOTHING TO RESTORE: YOUR CLOUD HAS %s, NOT %s.\n\nIS THE NAME RIGHT?").c_str(),
   53  				near.c_str(), folder.c_str()),
   54  			_("CHANGE FOLDER"), [window, folder] { GuiMenu::openCloudFolderEditor(window, folder); },
   55  			_("CREATE ANYWAY"), create,
   56  			_("NOT NOW"), nullptr));
   57  		return;
   58  	}
   59  	window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
   60  		folder.empty()
   61  			? _("YOUR CLOUD HAS NO SAVES FOLDER YET.\n\nCREATE IT NOW?")
   62  			: Utils::String::format(_("YOUR CLOUD HAS NO %s FOLDER YET.\n\nCREATE IT NOW?").c_str(), folder.c_str()),
   63  		_("CREATE IT"), create,
   64  		_("NOT NOW"), nullptr));
   65  }
   66  
   67  void present(Window* window, const std::string& offer, const std::vector<std::string>& args)
   68  {
   69  	if (window == nullptr || offer != "create-saves-folder")
   70  		return;
   71  
   72  	// The dialog is built on the interface thread wherever the answer came
   73  	// from: the card reads the script on a worker, the page reads it on a
   74  	// worker and asks from its own input handler, having just deleted
   75  	// itself. Posting covers both -- and on the page it also puts the
   76  	// dialog up on the frame after the one that took the press, rather than
   77  	// inside the input dispatch of a component that is gone. Through
   78  	// AppWindow: the card's worker is detached and asks after its linger,
   79  	// which can be after main() has let the window go (#308 8-es claude
   80  	// F-ES-26's rule).
   81  	AppWindow::post(window, [window, args]() { createSavesFolder(window, args); });
   82  }
   83  
   84  }
```

```text
    5  // A question a cloud script asked us to put to the player once its run is

    7  // buttons for it live.

    9  // Two surfaces run the same scripts: the card (ThreadedCloudSync), for the
   10  // quick save actions in GAME SETTINGS, and the page (GuiCloudTransfer), for
   11  // the long transfers under MANAGE CLOUD STORAGE. Both reach cloud_restore,
   12  // so both can be handed the same question -- and only the card ever asked
```

```text
   24  	Transform forGame(FileData* game)

   37  	// The content name RetroArch wrote into the file name is the ROM's
   38  	// file name without its extension. The game is looked for once per
   39  	// screenshot across the game systems -- a list of a few hundred games
   40  	// is a few hundred string compares, and the answer is kept, so moving
   41  	// the cursor back over a screenshot costs nothing. A screenshot whose
   42  	// game is in no list (a ROM since removed, a name RetroArch shortened)
   43  	// keeps the file's own proportions and turn.
   44  	// The screenshot -> game cache, and its lock: which system and which
   45  	// game path a screenshot's content name resolved to ("" when none),
   46  	// never the Transform itself. The turn is asked of CaptureRotation on
   47  	// every look, which stats the record, so a record written by a session
   48  	// or by hand over ssh is seen on the next look without a restart; the
   49  	// cache used to hold the whole Transform and kept the turn it was built
   50  	// with until the process ended (audit #258 PL-019). A FileData pointer
   51  	// is not cached either: the list that owns it is rebuilt by a rescan
   52  	// (#246's lesson), so the game is found again by path each time.
   53  	// forgetScreenshots empties it when a session records a rotation, which
   54  	// also covers a screenshot whose game arrived after the first look.
   55  	struct ShotGame { std::string system; std::string gamePath; };
   56  	static std::mutex sShotLock;
   57  	static std::map<std::string, ShotGame> sShots;
   58  	// The library by stem, built on the first screenshot that misses sShots
   59  	// and kept until forgetScreenshots: a list of S never-seen screenshots
   60  	// used to walk every game S times on the interface thread (audit #258,
   61  	// the seat's G-04); now the library is walked once. The first game of
   62  	// the first system to carry a stem wins, as the walk did.
   63  	static std::map<std::string, ShotGame> sStems;
   64  	static bool sStemsBuilt = false;
   65  
   66  	void forgetScreenshots()
   67  	{
   68  		std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(sShotLock);
   69  		sShots.clear();
   70  		sStems.clear();
   71  		sStemsBuilt = false;
   72  	}
   73  
   74  	static void buildStems()
   75  	{
   76  		sStems.clear();
   77  		for (SystemData* system : SystemData::sSystemVector)
   78  		{
   79  			if (system == nullptr || system->isCollection() || !system->isGameSystem() || system->getRootFolder() == nullptr)
   80  				continue;
   81  			if (system->hasPlatformId(PlatformIds::IMAGEVIEWER))
   82  				continue;
   83  			for (FileData* game : system->getRootFolder()->getFilesRecursive(GAME))
   84  				sStems.emplace(Utils::FileSystem::getStem(game->getPath()), ShotGame{ system->getName(), game->getPath() });
   85  		}
   86  		sStemsBuilt = true;
   87  	}

   89  	static FileData* findGame(const ShotGame& sg)
   90  	{
   91  		if (sg.system.empty())
   92  			return nullptr;
   93  		SystemData* system = SystemData::getSystem(sg.system);
   94  		if (system == nullptr || system->getRootFolder() == nullptr)
   95  			return nullptr;
   96  		return system->getRootFolder()->FindByPath(sg.gamePath);
   97  	}
   98  
   99  	Transform forScreenshotPath(const std::string& path)
  100  	{
  101  		{
  102  			std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(sShotLock);
  103  			auto it = sShots.find(path);
  104  			if (it != sShots.cend())
  105  			{
  106  				if (it->second.system.empty())
  107  					return Transform();
  108  				if (FileData* game = findGame(it->second))
  109  					return forGame(game);
  110  				// The list was rebuilt under it: look again below.
  111  			}
  112  		}
  113  		const std::string content = DisplayAspectText::screenshotContent(Utils::FileSystem::getFileName(path));
  114  		Transform t;
  115  		ShotGame sg;
  116  		std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(sShotLock);
  117  		if (!sStemsBuilt)
  118  			buildStems();
  119  		if (!content.empty())
  120  		{
  121  			auto it = sStems.find(content);
  122  			if (it != sStems.cend())
  123  			{
  124  				sg = it->second;
  125  				if (FileData* game = findGame(sg))
  126  					t = forGame(game);
  127  			}
  128  		}
  129  		sShots[path] = sg;
  130  		return t;
  131  	}
  132  
  133  	Transform forScreenshot(FileData* screenshot)
  134  	{
  135  		if (screenshot == nullptr)
  136  			return Transform();
  137  		return forScreenshotPath(screenshot->getPath());
  138  	}
  139  
  140  	void applyToBoundImages(GuiComponent* root, const std::string& imagePath, const Transform& transform)
  141  	{
  142  		if (root == nullptr)
  143  			return;
  144  		if (ImageComponent* image = dynamic_cast<ImageComponent*>(root))
  145  		{
  146  			const bool bound = !imagePath.empty() && image->getImagePath() == imagePath;
  147  			image->setDisplayAspect(bound ? transform.aspect : 0.0f);
  148  			image->setDisplayRotation(bound ? transform.turns : 0);
  149  		}
  150  		for (unsigned int i = 0; i < root->getChildCount(); i++)
  151  			applyToBoundImages(root->getChild(i), imagePath, transform);
  152  	}
  153  }
```

```text
    1  #include "DisplayAspectText.h"
    2  
    3  #include <cctype>
    4  #include <set>
    5  
    6  namespace DisplayAspectText
    7  {
    8  	float forSystem(const std::string& themeFolder)
    9  	{
   10  		// Consoles and computers whose cores emit non-square pixels and
   11  		// whose picture is a 4:3 screen: the NES's 256x240 and the SNES's
   12  		// 256x224 are both 4:3 on the screen they were made for; the Mega
   13  		// Drive's 320x224 and 256x224 likewise; the PC Engine's 256-wide
   14  		// modes; the 8-bit computers and consoles; the disc systems whose
   15  		// software cores render at native size. Handhelds are absent on
   16  		// purpose: their pixels are square and the file is right as it
   17  		// is. Arcade is absent too: a vertical shooter is 3:4.
   18  		static const std::set<std::string> fourByThree = {
   19  			"nes", "famicom", "fds", "nesh",
   20  			"snes", "sfc", "snesh", "snesmsu1", "satellaview", "sufami",
   21  			"megadrive", "genesis", "megadriveh", "genh", "megadrive-japan", "sega32x", "segacd", "megacd",
   22  			"mastersystem", "sg-1000",
   23  			"pcengine", "tg16", "supergrafx", "pcenginecd", "tg16cd", "pcfx",
   24  			"atari2600", "atari5200", "atari7800", "atari800", "atarist",
   25  			"c64", "c128", "vic20", "c16", "pet", "amiga", "amigacd32",
   26  			"msx", "msx2", "colecovision", "intellivision", "odyssey2", "videopac", "channelf",
   27  			"amstradcpc", "zxspectrum", "zx81", "x1", "x68000", "pc-8800", "pc-9800",
   28  			"psx", "saturn", "neogeo", "neocd", "cdi", "3do", "n64", "n64dd",
   29  		};
   30  		return fourByThree.count(themeFolder) ? 4.0f / 3.0f : 0.0f;
   31  	}
   32  
   33  	std::string screenshotContent(const std::string& fileName)
   34  	{
   35  		// Two shapes RetroArch writes: "<content>-YYMMDD-HHMMSS" for a
   36  		// screenshot the player took (twelve digits in two groups of six
   37  		// after the last two dashes), and "<content>-cheevo-<id>" for the
   38  		// one it takes at an achievement unlock (fork #248: the
   39  		// maintainer's SCREENSHOTS folder was mostly the second kind).
   40  		// With or without the extension -- an image's: the part after the
   41  		// last dot of an extensionless "Dr. Mario-260922-153012" is the
   42  		// content's, and taking it for an extension left "Dr" (#308 8a gpt
   43  		// F-ES-21).
   44  		std::string stem = fileName;
   45  		const size_t dot = stem.rfind('.');
   46  		if (dot != std::string::npos && dot > 0)
   47  		{
   48  			std::string ext = stem.substr(dot + 1);
   49  			for (auto& c : ext)
   50  				c = (char) tolower((unsigned char) c);
   51  			static const std::set<std::string> images = { "png", "jpg", "jpeg", "bmp", "gif", "webp" };
   52  			if (images.count(ext))
   53  				stem = stem.substr(0, dot);
   54  		}
   55  		const size_t cheevo = stem.rfind("-cheevo-");
   56  		if (cheevo != std::string::npos && cheevo > 0 && cheevo + 8 < stem.size())
   57  		{
   58  			bool digits = true;
   59  			for (size_t i = cheevo + 8; i < stem.size(); i++)
   60  				if (!isdigit((unsigned char) stem[i]))
   61  					digits = false;
   62  			if (digits)
   63  				return stem.substr(0, cheevo);
   64  		}
   65  		if (stem.size() < 15)
   66  			return "";
   67  		const std::string tail = stem.substr(stem.size() - 14);
   68  		if (tail[0] != '-' || tail[7] != '-')
   69  			return "";
   70  		for (size_t i = 1; i < 14; i++)
   71  			if (i != 7 && !isdigit((unsigned char) tail[i]))
   72  				return "";
   73  		const std::string content = stem.substr(0, stem.size() - 14);
   74  		return content;
   75  	}
   76  }
```

## es-app/src/FolderMerge.h @ 16dbac5211 (whole, 74 lines)

```text
    1  #pragma once
    2  #ifndef ES_APP_FOLDER_MERGE_H
    3  #define ES_APP_FOLDER_MERGE_H
    4  
    5  // A folder re-read from disk, folded into the tree already in memory
    6  // (audit #307 PL-014; SystemData::rescanIfFolderChanged).
    7  //
    8  // The rescan deleted every FileData of the system and read the folder
    9  // again: clear(), then populateFolder. Every holder of one of those
   10  // pointers -- the collections' entries (recently played, favorites, all
   11  // games, the custom ones), a group system's folder for its grouped child,
   12  // a hasher's queue -- then held freed memory, for files that had not
   13  // changed at all. The view was one such holder, and was fixed (#246); the
   14  // rest were not. So the rescan reads the folder into a tree of its own and
   15  // merges: an entry still on disk keeps the object it had, an entry only on
   16  // disk moves in, an entry gone from disk is handed to the caller, who drops
   17  // every holder of it and deletes it. Only what vanished from disk is ever
   18  // deleted, which is what a player deleting a game already does.
   19  //
   20  // Generic, so the rule has a test without a window: Tree supplies
   21  //   std::vector<Node*> children(Node* folder)   a copy
   22  //   std::string key(Node* n)                    its full path
   23  //   bool isFolder(Node* n)
   24  //   void detach(Node* folder, Node* n)          out of folder, not deleted
   25  //   void attach(Node* folder, Node* n)          into folder, parent set
   26  //   void vanished(Node* n)                      in memory, gone from disk
   27  //   void arrived(Node* n)                       moved in from the fresh tree
   28  // Afterwards `fresh` holds only duplicates of what `live` kept, never seen
   29  // by anything, for the caller to delete.
   30  
   31  #include <string>
   32  #include <unordered_map>
   33  #include <vector>
   34  
   35  namespace FolderMerge
   36  {
   37  	template <class Node, class Tree>
   38  	void merge(Node* live, Node* fresh, Tree& tree)
   39  	{
   40  		// What is on disk at this level, by path.
   41  		std::unordered_map<std::string, Node*> onDisk;
   42  		for (Node* f : tree.children(fresh))
   43  			onDisk[tree.key(f)] = f;
   44  
   45  		// Gone from disk, or a file that is a folder now (or the reverse):
   46  		// the caller's to drop and delete. Everything else stays as it is.
   47  		std::unordered_map<std::string, Node*> kept;
   48  		for (Node* l : tree.children(live))
   49  		{
   50  			auto it = onDisk.find(tree.key(l));
   51  			if (it == onDisk.end() || tree.isFolder(it->second) != tree.isFolder(l))
   52  				tree.vanished(l);
   53  			else
   54  				kept[tree.key(l)] = l;
   55  		}
   56  
   57  		// New on disk: moved in whole, a new folder with everything in it.
   58  		// A folder both sides have is merged the same way, one level down.
   59  		for (Node* f : tree.children(fresh))
   60  		{
   61  			auto it = kept.find(tree.key(f));
   62  			if (it == kept.end())
   63  			{
   64  				tree.detach(fresh, f);
   65  				tree.attach(live, f);
   66  				tree.arrived(f);
   67  			}
   68  			else if (tree.isFolder(f))
   69  				merge(it->second, f, tree);
   70  		}
   71  	}
   72  }
   73  
   74  #endif // ES_APP_FOLDER_MERGE_H
```


## es-app/src/GameCubeRvzHashRetry.h @ 16dbac5211 (whole, 45 lines)

```text
    1  #pragma once
    2  // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
    3  // #574: narrow recovery of failed frontend RVZ hashes, not metadata cleanup.
    4  #include <string>
    5  
    6  namespace GameCubeRvzHashRetry
    7  {
    8      inline std::string trim(const std::string& value)
    9      {
   10          const auto first = value.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n");
   11          if (first == std::string::npos)
   12              return {};
   13          return value.substr(first, value.find_last_not_of(" \t\r\n") - first + 1);
   14      }
   15  
   16      inline bool needed(bool readerEnabled, bool gameCubeOnly,
   17          const std::string& extension, const std::string& storedHash, const std::string& storedId)
   18      {
   19          if (!readerEnabled || !gameCubeOnly || extension.size() != 4 || extension[0] != '.' ||
   20              (extension[1] != 'r' && extension[1] != 'R') ||
   21              (extension[2] != 'v' && extension[2] != 'V') ||
   22              (extension[3] != 'z' && extension[3] != 'Z'))
   23              return false;
   24  
   25          // Only an absent ID or the existing no-ID marker is eligible. Preserve
   26          // positive IDs and unfamiliar nonempty metadata, even beside a bad hash.
   27          const std::string id = trim(storedId);
   28          if (!id.empty() && id != "0")
   29              return false;
   30  
   31          const std::string hash = trim(storedHash);
   32          if (hash.size() != 32)
   33              return true;
   34          bool nonzero = false;
   35          for (char c : hash)
   36          {
   37              if (!((c >= '0' && c <= '9') || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'F')))
   38                  return true;
   39              nonzero = nonzero || c != '0';
   40          }
   41          // A valid unknown hash still takes the old lookup-only path. The all-
   42          // zero value is getCheevosHashFromFile's historical failure sentinel.
   43          return !nonzero;
   44      }
   45  }
```

## es-app/src/JourneyTiers.h @ 16dbac5211 (whole, 159 lines)

```text
    1  #pragma once
    2  #ifndef ES_APP_JOURNEY_TIERS_H
    3  #define ES_APP_JOURNEY_TIERS_H
    4  
    5  // What a settings-first restore carries across its restart (audit #307
    6  // PL-029). RESTORE with SETTINGS ticked restores the settings, restarts,
    7  // and at the next start offers the rest: the restore form's dialog says
    8  // ANYTHING ELSE YOU TICKED IS RESTORED AFTER THE RESTART. The ticks cannot
    9  // ride in system.cfg -- the settings restore is what replaces it -- so the
   10  // form writes them here, beside the content picker's selection in
   11  // /storage/.cache/cloud_sync, which the settings backup deliberately does
   12  // not capture; the start reads them back when backuptool's journey marker
   13  // is there, and builds the continuation from them.
   14  //
   15  // Pure: the record's text and the continuation's command. The file and the
   16  // dialogs are GuiMenu's and main's.
   17  
   18  #include <string>
   19  #include <vector>
   20  
   21  namespace JourneyTiers
   22  {
   23  	// Where the ticks wait for the restart.
   24  	constexpr const char* PATH = "/storage/.cache/cloud_sync/journey-tiers";
   25  
   26  	struct Tiers
   27  	{
   28  		bool known = false;    // a record this build's form wrote, read whole
   29  		bool saves = false;
   30  		bool content = false;  // ROMs and BIOS
   31  		bool media = false;    // game content
   32  		bool any() const { return saves || content || media; }
   33  	};
   34  
   35  	// The record, a line that says what it is and then one line a tier
   36  	// (es-code-traps.md: a record the interface writes and reads back is a
   37  	// line that says what it is, never a bare digit).
   38  	inline std::string record(bool saves, bool content, bool media)
   39  	{
   40  		return std::string("journey-tiers=1\n")
   41  			+ "saves=" + (saves ? "1" : "0") + "\n"
   42  			+ "content=" + (content ? "1" : "0") + "\n"
   43  			+ "media=" + (media ? "1" : "0") + "\n";
   44  	}
   45  
   46  	// known only when the first line says journey-tiers=1 and the three
   47  	// tiers follow, each 0 or 1: anything else -- no file, an empty one, one
   48  	// cut short, a later format -- is not this build's record, and the
   49  	// continuation falls back to the one the marker always meant. Keys this
   50  	// build does not know are passed over.
   51  	inline Tiers parse(const std::string& text)
   52  	{
   53  		Tiers t;
   54  		std::vector<std::string> lines;
   55  		std::string line;
   56  		for (char c : text)
   57  		{
   58  			if (c == '\n') { lines.push_back(line); line.clear(); }
   59  			else if (c != '\r') line += c;
   60  		}
   61  		if (!line.empty())
   62  			lines.push_back(line);
   63  		if (lines.empty() || lines[0] != "journey-tiers=1")
   64  			return t;
   65  		// Whole, or not known (audit of the fixes, E2 gpt G-E2-02): all
   66  		// three tiers there, each 0 or 1. A record cut short after its
   67  		// first line read as one naming nothing, and the start consumed the
   68  		// marker without offering anything; not known, it offers everything
   69  		// and says so -- what a marker with no record has always meant.
   70  		int seen[3] = { 0, 0, 0 };
   71  		bool* tier[3] = { &t.saves, &t.content, &t.media };
   72  		const char* key[3] = { "saves=", "content=", "media=" };
   73  		for (size_t i = 1; i < lines.size(); i++)
   74  			for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++)
   75  			{
   76  				const std::string prefix = key[k];
   77  				if (lines[i].compare(0, prefix.size(), prefix) != 0)
   78  					continue;
   79  				const std::string value = lines[i].substr(prefix.size());
   80  				if (value != "0" && value != "1")
   81  					return Tiers();
   82  				*tier[k] = value == "1";
   83  				seen[k]++;
   84  			}
   85  		if (seen[0] != 1 || seen[1] != 1 || seen[2] != 1)
   86  			return Tiers();
   87  		t.known = true;
   88  		return t;
   89  	}
   90  
   91  	// Writing the record for a new restore: what is at PATH afterwards.
   92  	//   Written          this restore's ticks
   93  	//   NoRecord         none -- the start offers everything, and its prompt
   94  	//                    names everything
   95  	//   OldRecordStands  an earlier attempt's record, which could be neither
   96  	//                    replaced nor removed: the restore must not start
   97  	// The file operations are handed in, so the rule has a case without a
   98  	// card to fail.
   99  	enum class Replaced { Written, NoRecord, OldRecordStands };
  100  	// (Audit of the fixes, E2 gpt G-E2-01: a write that failed used to be
  101  	// logged and passed over, and an earlier attempt's record at the same
  102  	// path was then read as this restore's.) The old record goes first; a
  103  	// write that fails then leaves none.
  104  	template <class Write, class Remove, class Exists>
  105  	Replaced replaceRecord(const std::string& text, Write write, Remove remove, Exists exists)
  106  	{
  107  		if (exists())
  108  			remove();
  109  		if (exists())
  110  			return Replaced::OldRecordStands;
  111  		if (write(text))
  112  			return Replaced::Written;
  113  		// A write that failed part-way may have left something: gone, or
  114  		// the restore does not start.
  115  		if (exists() && !remove())
  116  			return Replaced::OldRecordStands;
  117  		return exists() ? Replaced::OldRecordStands : Replaced::NoRecord;
  118  	}
  119  
  120  	// The continuation a journey marker offers: every part as its own tier,
  121  	// each reporting itself as it ends (">>> tier <label>|<rc>", the label
  122  	// being what the restore form calls the item) and the run's status
  123  	// accumulated rather than taken from the last part -- the restore
  124  	// form's composition (GuiMenu, cloudOpenTransfer), with the parts the
  125  	// player ticked and none they did not. Saves first, then the content
  126  	// the picker's selection names (--selected; the picker wrote it with
  127  	// --set-systems before the restore ran, into the same directory as the
  128  	// record, so the settings restore did not touch it either).
  129  	//
  130  	// A marker with no record of this build's (unknown) is one an earlier
  131  	// build left (D-WORKFLOW-050, read both): its continuation was
  132  	// everything, and its prompt said so, so that is what it still runs.
  133  	inline std::string command(const Tiers& t)
  134  	{
  135  		std::string cmd = "rc=0";
  136  		auto add = [&cmd](const std::string& label, const std::string& part)
  137  		{
  138  			cmd += " ; _t=0 ; { " + part + " ; } || _t=$? ; echo \">>> tier " + label + "|$_t\" ; [ \"$_t\" = 0 ] || rc=$_t";
  139  		};
  140  		if (!t.known)
  141  		{
  142  			add("RESTORING ROMS AND BIOS", "/usr/bin/cloud_content_restore --all");
  143  			add("RESTORING SAVES", "/usr/bin/cloud_restore --yes");
  144  		}
  145  		else
  146  		{
  147  			if (t.saves)
  148  				add("SAVES", "/usr/bin/cloud_restore --yes --saves-only");
  149  			if (t.content || t.media)
  150  				add(t.content && t.media ? "ROMS, BIOS, AND GAME CONTENT" : t.content ? "ROMS AND BIOS" : "GAME CONTENT",
  151  					std::string("/usr/bin/cloud_content_restore --selected")
  152  					+ (t.content && t.media ? " --with-media" : t.media ? " --media-only" : ""));
  153  		}
  154  		cmd += " ; exit $rc";
  155  		return cmd;
  156  	}
  157  }
  158  
  159  #endif // ES_APP_JOURNEY_TIERS_H
```

```text
   10  	int jsonInt(const rapidjson::Value& val, const char* name)
   11  	{
   12  		if (!val.IsObject() || !val.HasMember(name))
   13  			return 0;
   14  		const rapidjson::Value& v = val[name];
   15  		if (v.IsInt())
   16  			return v.GetInt();
   17  		if (v.IsInt64())
   18  			return (int)v.GetInt64();
   19  		if (v.IsString())
   20  			return Utils::String::toInteger(v.GetString());
   21  		return 0;
   22  	}
   23  
   24  	std::string jsonString(const rapidjson::Value& val, const char* name)
   25  	{
   26  		if (!val.IsObject() || !val.HasMember(name))
   27  			return "";
   28  		const rapidjson::Value& v = val[name];
   29  		if (v.IsString())
   30  			return v.GetString();
   31  		if (v.IsInt())
   32  			return std::to_string(v.GetInt());
   33  		return "";
   34  	}

   36  	// A core achievement is Flags 3; 5 is unofficial and RetroArch shows it

   41  	void readAchievements(const rapidjson::Value& list, std::vector<OfflineAchievementsText::Achievement>& out)
   42  	{
   43  		if (!list.IsArray())
   44  			return;
   45  		for (const auto& item : list.GetArray())
   46  		{
   47  			if (!item.IsObject())
   48  				continue;
   49  			OfflineAchievementsText::Achievement a;
   50  			a.id = jsonInt(item, "ID");
   51  			if (a.id <= 0)
   52  				continue;
   53  			if (item.HasMember("Flags") && jsonInt(item, "Flags") != FlagCore)
   54  				continue;
   55  			a.title = jsonString(item, "Title");
   56  			a.description = jsonString(item, "Description");

   71  	// One whole non-negative number and nothing else.
   72  	bool wholeNumber(const std::string& s)
   73  	{
   74  		if (s.empty())
   75  			return false;
   76  		for (char c : s)
   77  			if (c < '0' || c > '9')

   95  OfflineAchievementsText::Game OfflineAchievementsText::parsePatch(const std::string& body)
   96  {
   97  	Game game;
   98  	rapidjson::Document doc;
   99  	if (!parseDocument(body, doc) || !doc.HasMember("PatchData") || !doc["PatchData"].IsObject())
  100  		return game;
  101  
  102  	const rapidjson::Value& patch = doc["PatchData"];
  103  	game.id = jsonInt(patch, "ID");
  104  	if (game.id <= 0)
  105  		return game;
  106  	game.title = jsonString(patch, "Title");
  107  	game.imageUrl = jsonString(patch, "ImageIcon");
  108  	// A set with nothing in it is not a cached game (PL-26).
  109  	if (!patch.HasMember("Achievements") || !patch["Achievements"].IsArray() || patch["Achievements"].Empty())
  110  		return game;
  111  	readAchievements(patch["Achievements"], game.achievements);
  112  	game.ok = true;
  113  	return game;
  114  }
  115  
  116  OfflineAchievementsText::Game OfflineAchievementsText::parseAchievementSets(const std::string& body)
  117  {
  118  	Game game;
  119  	rapidjson::Document doc;
  120  	if (!parseDocument(body, doc))
  121  		return game;
  122  
  123  	game.id = jsonInt(doc, "GameId");
  124  	if (game.id <= 0)
  125  		return game;
  126  	game.title = jsonString(doc, "Title");
  127  	game.imageUrl = jsonString(doc, "ImageIconUrl");
  128  
  129  	if (!doc.HasMember("Sets") || !doc["Sets"].IsArray())
  130  		return game;
  131  	const rapidjson::Value* chosen = nullptr;
  132  	for (const auto& set : doc["Sets"].GetArray())
  133  	{
  134  		if (!set.IsObject())
  135  			continue;
  136  		if (chosen == nullptr)
  137  			chosen = &set;
  138  		if (jsonString(set, "Type") == "core")
  139  		{
  140  			chosen = &set;
  141  			break;
  142  		}
  143  	}
  144  	// No set, or a set with nothing in it, is not a cached game (PL-26).
  145  	if (chosen == nullptr || !chosen->HasMember("Achievements") || !(*chosen)["Achievements"].IsArray() || (*chosen)["Achievements"].Empty())
  146  		return game;
  147  	readAchievements((*chosen)["Achievements"], game.achievements);
  148  	game.ok = true;
  149  	return game;
  150  }
  151  
  152  OfflineAchievementsText::StoreGame OfflineAchievementsText::parseStoreGame(const std::string& line)
  153  {
  154  	StoreGame game;
  155  	rapidjson::Document doc;
  156  	if (!parseDocument(line, doc) || !doc.IsObject())
  157  		return game;
  158  	game.id = jsonInt(doc, "id");
  159  	game.achievements = jsonInt(doc, "achievements");
  160  	if (game.id <= 0 || game.achievements <= 0)
  161  		return game;
  162  	game.title = jsonString(doc, "title");
  163  	game.icon = jsonString(doc, "icon");
  164  	game.points = jsonInt(doc, "points");
  165  	// null is the ctl's "the unlock row could not be read" (#307 PL-057):
  166  	// unknown, not none. A count left out altogether still reads 0.
  167  	if (doc.HasMember("unlocked") && doc["unlocked"].IsNull())
  168  		game.unlockedKnown = false;
  169  	else
  170  	{
  171  		game.unlocked = jsonInt(doc, "unlocked");
  172  		game.unlockedPoints = jsonInt(doc, "unlockedPoints");
  173  	}
  174  	game.pending = jsonInt(doc, "pending");
  175  	game.ok = true;
  176  	return game;
  177  }
  178  
  179  OfflineAchievementsText::Unlocks OfflineAchievementsText::parseUnlocks(const std::string& body)
  180  {
  181  	Unlocks unlocks;
  182  	rapidjson::Document doc;
  183  	if (!parseDocument(body, doc) || !doc.HasMember("UserUnlocks") || !doc["UserUnlocks"].IsArray())
  184  		return unlocks;
  185  	// Success false with a list would be the proxy contradicting itself;
  186  	// not the shape either.
  187  	if (doc.HasMember("Success") && doc["Success"].IsBool() && !doc["Success"].GetBool())
  188  		return unlocks;
  189  	for (const auto& v : doc["UserUnlocks"].GetArray())
  190  	{
  191  		int id = 0;
  192  		if (v.IsInt())
  193  			id = v.GetInt();
  194  		else if (v.IsString())
  195  			id = Utils::String::toInteger(v.GetString());
  196  		if (id > 0)
  197  			unlocks.ids.push_back(id);
  198  	}
  199  	unlocks.ok = true;
  200  	return unlocks;
  201  }

  204  {

  218  std::vector<OfflineAchievementsText::PendingAward> OfflineAchievementsText::parsePendingIds(const std::string& text)
  219  {
  220  	std::vector<PendingAward> out;
  221  	for (const std::string& raw : Utils::String::split(text, '\n', true))
  222  	{
  223  		const std::string line = Utils::String::trim(raw);
  224  		if (line.empty())
  225  			continue;
  226  		auto fields = Utils::String::split(line, ' ', true);
  227  		if (fields.size() != 2 || !wholeNumber(fields[0]) || !wholeNumber(fields[1]))
  228  			continue;
  229  		PendingAward award;
  230  		award.id = Utils::String::toInteger(fields[0]);
  231  		award.when = (time_t)strtoll(fields[1].c_str(), nullptr, 10);
  232  		if (award.id <= 0)
  233  			continue;
  234  		out.push_back(award);
  235  	}
  236  	return out;
  237  }
  238  
  239  bool OfflineAchievementsText::parseOnlineState(const std::string& text, bool& online)
  240  {
  241  	rapidjson::Document doc;
  242  	if (!parseDocument(text, doc) || !doc.HasMember("online") || !doc["online"].IsBool())
  243  		return false;
  244  	online = doc["online"].GetBool();
  245  	return true;
  246  }
  247  
  248  std::vector<int> OfflineAchievementsText::parseReadyIds(const std::string& text)
  249  {
  250  	std::vector<int> ids;
  251  	for (const std::string& raw : Utils::String::split(text, '\n', true))
  252  	{
  253  		const std::string line = Utils::String::trim(raw);
  254  		if (!wholeNumber(line))
  255  			continue;
  256  		int id = Utils::String::toInteger(line);
  257  		if (id > 0)
  258  			ids.push_back(id);
  259  	}
  260  	return ids;
  261  }
  262  
  263  OfflineAchievementsText::AccountTotals OfflineAchievementsText::parseAccountTotals(const std::string& text)
  264  {
  265  	AccountTotals totals;
  266  	bool score = false, softcore = false;
  267  	for (const std::string& raw : Utils::String::split(Utils::String::trim(text), ' ', true))
  268  	{
  269  		const std::string field = Utils::String::trim(raw);
  270  		if (Utils::String::startsWith(field, "score=") && wholeNumber(field.substr(6)))
  271  		{
  272  			totals.score = Utils::String::toInteger(field.substr(6));
  273  			score = true;
  274  		}
  275  		else if (Utils::String::startsWith(field, "softcore=") && wholeNumber(field.substr(9)))
  276  		{
  277  			totals.softcore = Utils::String::toInteger(field.substr(9));
  278  			softcore = true;
  279  		}
  280  	}
  281  	totals.ok = score && softcore;
  282  	return totals;
  283  }
```

```text
    1  #pragma once
    2  #ifndef ES_APP_OFFLINE_ACHIEVEMENTS_TEXT_H
    3  #define ES_APP_OFFLINE_ACHIEVEMENTS_TEXT_H
    4  
    5  // What the offline RetroAchievements proxy answers, read as text (fork
    6  // #180, D-RA-009). With OFFLINE ACHIEVEMENTS on and the device offline, the
    7  // achievements pages read the proxy's on-device cache through the same
    8  // dorequest.php actions RetroArch uses, and this is the pure reading of
    9  // those answers -- the shapes captured from a guest's proxy on 2026-09-14:
   10  //
   11  //   patch            {"Success":true,"PatchData":{"ID","Title","ImageIcon",
   12  //                    "Achievements":[{"ID","Title","Description","Points",
   13  //                    "BadgeName","Flags","BadgeURL","BadgeLockedURL",...}]}}
   14  //                    -- a game the scan cached, keyed by game id;
   15  //   achievementsets  {"Success":true,"GameId","Title","ImageIconUrl",
   16  //                    "Sets":[{"Type":"core","Achievements":[same]}]}
   17  //                    -- a game started once through RetroArch, keyed by hash;
   18  //   unlocks          {"Success":true,"UserUnlocks":[100359]} -- the cached
   19  //                    unlocks merged with the awards still queued; an unknown
   20  //                    game answers 200 with an empty list, so this never
   21  //                    decides whether a game is cached;
   22  //   a miss           503 {"Success":false,"Error":"no cached response"}.
   23  //
   24  // Nothing here reads a file, asks SystemConf, opens a socket or measures a
   25  // font, so es-unit-tests checks it (es-app/tests/unit). OfflineAchievements
   26  // does the asking; RetroAchievements turns the model into the page's.
   27  //
   28  // Translation stays outside on purpose: no _() here.
   29  
   30  #include <ctime>
   31  #include <string>
   32  #include <vector>
   33  
   34  namespace OfflineAchievementsText
   35  {
   36  	// The proxy's address on the device is Utils::OfflineProxy::Base
   37  	// (es-core/src/utils/OfflineProxyUrl.h): RetroArch is pointed at it per
   38  	// launch by setsettings.sh, the interface asks the same, and
   39  	// WebImageComponent recognises a request to it by that one definition
   40  	// (fork #199).
   41  
   42  	// A dorequest.php URL on the proxy for a query string ("r=patch&g=1").
   43  	std::string requestUrl(const std::string& query);
   44  
   45  	// A badge image on the proxy, for an achievement whose body carried no
   46  	// URL: /Badge/<name>.png, or the _lock variant.
   47  	std::string badgeUrl(const std::string& badgeName, bool unlocked);
   48  
   49  	struct Achievement
   50  	{
   51  		int id = 0;
   52  		std::string title;
   53  		std::string description;
   54  		int points = 0;
   55  		std::string badgeName;
   56  		std::string badgeUrl;        // as the proxy rewrote it; empty when the body had none
   57  		std::string badgeLockedUrl;
   58  	};
   59  
   60  	struct Game
   61  	{
   62  		bool ok = false;             // a body of the right shape with a positive game id
   63  		int id = 0;
   64  		std::string title;
   65  		std::string imageUrl;        // the game's icon, as the proxy rewrote it
   66  		std::vector<Achievement> achievements;
   67  	};
   68  
   69  	// The patch action's body. Keeps the core achievements (Flags 3) with a
   70  	// positive id, in the order the body gives them, which is the server's
   71  	// display order. ok is false for anything else -- a miss, junk, a body
   72  	// without PatchData, and a body whose Achievements is missing, not a
   73  	// list, or empty: the proxy caches a game for its set, so a set with
   74  	// nothing in it is not the shape, and must not become a page of "0 of
   75  	// 0" (audit #186 PL-26, guards fail closed). A set whose every entry the
   76  	// filter drops (all unofficial) is still the shape, with no achievements.
   77  	Game parsePatch(const std::string& body);
   78  
   79  	// The achievementsets action's body: the core set (Type "core"), or the
   80  	// first set when none says so. Same filter and the same rule for an
   81  	// empty set as parsePatch.
   82  	Game parseAchievementSets(const std::string& body);
   83  
   84  	// The ids in UserUnlocks. ok is true only for a body of that shape --
   85  	// Success true and UserUnlocks a list -- and the list may then be empty:
   86  	// an unknown game and a cached game nobody has unlocked anything in both
   87  	// answer 200 with none, so emptiness never decides whether a game is
   88  	// cached. Any other body is not ok, and the caller must not read it as
   89  	// "nothing unlocked" (audit #186 PL-26): validity travels apart from
   90  	// emptiness.
   91  	struct Unlocks
   92  	{
   93  		bool ok = false;
   94  		std::vector<int> ids;
   95  	};
   96  	Unlocks parseUnlocks(const std::string& body);
   97  
   98  	// The Error of a body that says Success false, else empty. The proxy
   99  	// answers a miss with 503 and this body; HttpReq hands the body back as
  100  	// the error message, and this is how the interface tells "not cached"
  101  	// from "the proxy is not answering".
  102  	std::string parseError(const std::string& body);
  103  	bool isNotCached(const std::string& body);
  104  
  105  	// "<achievementId> <epoch>" per line from raofflineproxy-ctl pending-ids:
  106  	// the casual awards still waiting for a connection, and when each was
  107  	// earned. Lines of any other shape are dropped.
  108  	struct PendingAward
  109  	{
  110  		int id = 0;
  111  		time_t when = 0;
  112  	};
  113  	std::vector<PendingAward> parsePendingIds(const std::string& text);
  114  
  115  	// One line of raofflineproxy-ctl summary: a game the store holds for the
  116  	// account, with what the summary page shows of it, from one read of the
  117  	// store (fork #190). ok is false for anything but a JSON object with a
  118  	// positive id and a set of at least one achievement; the other counts read
  119  	// 0 when absent. The icon is a URL the proxy serves, as the ctl wrote it.
  120  	struct StoreGame
  121  	{
  122  		bool ok = false;
  123  		int id = 0;
  124  		std::string title;
  125  		std::string icon;
  126  		int achievements = 0;
  127  		int points = 0;
  128  		int unlocked = 0;
  129  		int unlockedPoints = 0;
  130  		// False when the ctl said null: the store's unlock row for the game
  131  		// is missing or cannot be read, and unlocked is not a count of none
  132  		// (#307 PL-057). unlocked and unlockedPoints read 0 then, and mean
  133  		// nothing.
  134  		bool unlockedKnown = true;
  135  		int pending = 0;
  136  	};
  137  	StoreGame parseStoreGame(const std::string& line);
  138  
  139  	// The proxy's own online_state.json ({"online": false}): true when the
  140  	// text is one, with online set; false for anything else, and the caller
  141  	// treats a state it cannot read as unknown, never as offline.
  142  	bool parseOnlineState(const std::string& text, bool& online);
  143  
  144  	// The client's cached_game_ids.txt, one id per line: the games the proxy
  145  	// holds achievement data for. A line that is not a whole number is not a
  146  	// game.
  147  	std::vector<int> parseReadyIds(const std::string& text);
  148  
  149  	// "score=<n> softcore=<n>" from raofflineproxy-ctl account: the account's
  150  	// points as RetroAchievements last told the proxy. ok is false for a line
  151  	// of any other shape -- a number that was not printed is never shown.
  152  	struct AccountTotals
  153  	{
  154  		bool ok = false;
  155  		int score = 0;
  156  		int softcore = 0;
  157  	};
  158  	AccountTotals parseAccountTotals(const std::string& text);
  159  }
  160  
  161  #endif // ES_APP_OFFLINE_ACHIEVEMENTS_TEXT_H
```

## es-app/src/RunLock.h @ 16dbac5211 (whole, 74 lines)

```text
    1  #pragma once
    2  #ifndef ES_APP_RUN_LOCK_H
    3  #define ES_APP_RUN_LOCK_H
    4  
    5  // Who holds a script's run lock (#308 1-raoffline claude F-RA-08, gpt
    6  // F-RA-16). raofflineproxy-ctl takes an flock on its lock file for a run and
    7  // writes its pid into it; the file stays, pid and all, after the run ends,
    8  // and a run killed -9 leaves it too. So the pid in the file names a run
    9  // only while the lock is held -- the ctl's own stop path asks the lock first
   10  // (stop_running_run: flock -n) -- and, since the pid is written just after
   11  // the lock is taken, only when the process under it is the program that
   12  // takes it. OfflineAchievements::stopRun signals what this returns.
   13  
   14  #include <cstdlib>
   15  #include <fcntl.h>
   16  #include <fstream>
   17  #include <iterator>
   18  #include <string>
   19  #include <sys/file.h>
   20  #include <unistd.h>
   21  
   22  namespace RunLock
   23  {
   24  	// Whether a process holds an flock on `path` right now -- the cloud
   25  	// scripts' /var/run/cloud_sync.lock while a transfer runs (engineering-
   26  	// practices.md: the file stays behind after every run, so its existence
   27  	// means nothing). A shared lock taken for an instant fails while one is
   28  	// held, and is let go at once. No file is nobody's lock.
   29  	inline bool held(const std::string& lockPath)
   30  	{
   31  		const int fd = ::open(lockPath.c_str(), O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
   32  		if (fd < 0)
   33  			return false;
   34  		const bool free = ::flock(fd, LOCK_SH | LOCK_NB) == 0;
   35  		if (free)
   36  			::flock(fd, LOCK_UN);
   37  		::close(fd);
   38  		return !free;
   39  	}
   40  
   41  	// The pid holding `lockPath`, when its command line names `program`;
   42  	// 0 when nobody holds the lock, the file names no pid, or the pid is
   43  	// some other process.
   44  	inline long holder(const std::string& lockPath, const std::string& program)
   45  	{
   46  		const int fd = ::open(lockPath.c_str(), O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
   47  		if (fd < 0)
   48  			return 0;
   49  		// Taken shared for an instant and let go: a lock nobody holds is a
   50  		// file a finished run left. The ctl's own check does the same.
   51  		if (::flock(fd, LOCK_SH | LOCK_NB) == 0)
   52  		{
   53  			::flock(fd, LOCK_UN);
   54  			::close(fd);
   55  			return 0;
   56  		}
   57  		char buf[32];
   58  		const ssize_t n = ::pread(fd, buf, sizeof(buf) - 1, 0);
   59  		::close(fd);
   60  		if (n <= 0)
   61  			return 0;
   62  		buf[n] = '\0';
   63  		const long pid = std::strtol(buf, nullptr, 10);
   64  		if (pid <= 1)
   65  			return 0;
   66  		std::ifstream cmd("/proc/" + std::to_string(pid) + "/cmdline", std::ios::binary);
   67  		const std::string line((std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(cmd)), std::istreambuf_iterator<char>());
   68  		if (line.find(program) == std::string::npos)
   69  			return 0;
   70  		return pid;
   71  	}
   72  }
   73
```

## es-app/src/TextFit.h @ 16dbac5211 (whole, 40 lines)

```text
    1  #pragma once
    2  #ifndef ES_APP_TEXT_FIT_H
    3  #define ES_APP_TEXT_FIT_H
    4  
    5  // One line of text clipped to a width with an ellipsis: the long-job pages'
    6  // fitting (GuiCloudTransfer, GuiOfflineScan), measured by the caller's font.
    7  //
    8  // Cut on characters, not bytes (#308 1-raoffline gpt F-RA-23,
    9  // 5-cloud-sync-and-saves gpt F-CS-32). Both pages keep a name's UTF-8
   10  // (es-code-traps.md: rclone shortens with U+2026, a ROM is named in any
   11  // script), and the loop popped one byte at a time: a name ending in an
   12  // accented or Japanese character was measured, and could be shown, with
   13  // half a character on its end.
   14  #include <string>
   15  
   16  namespace TextFit
   17  {
   18  	// Drop the last character: its lead byte and every continuation byte.
   19  	inline void popCharacter(std::string& text)
   20  	{
   21  		if (text.empty())
   22  			return;
   23  		size_t i = text.size() - 1;
   24  		while (i > 0 && (static_cast<unsigned char>(text[i]) & 0xC0) == 0x80)
   25  			i--;
   26  		text.erase(i);
   27  	}
   28  
   29  	template <class Measure>
   30  	std::string fitOneLine(std::string text, float width, Measure measure)
   31  	{
   32  		if (text.empty() || measure(text) <= width)
   33  			return text;
   34  		while (text.size() > 4 && measure(text + "...") > width)
   35  			popCharacter(text);
   36  		return text + "...";
   37  	}
   38  }
   39  
   40  #endif // ES_APP_TEXT_FIT_H
```

```text
    1  #include "WifiText.h"
    2  
    3  // Only a CR: the name is the rest of the line, spaces and all.
    4  static std::string withoutCR(const std::string& line)
    5  {
    6  	if (!line.empty() && line.back() == '\r')
    7  		return line.substr(0, line.size() - 1);
    8  	return line;
    9  }
   10  
   11  bool WifiText::parseSavedLine(const std::string& rawLine, SavedNetwork& network)
   12  {
   13  	const std::string line = withoutCR(rawLine);
   14  	const size_t tab = line.rfind('\t');
   15  	if (tab == std::string::npos)
   16  		return false;
   17  
   18  	const std::string name = line.substr(0, tab);
   19  	const std::string flag = line.substr(tab + 1);
   20  	if (name.empty())
   21  		return false;
   22  
   23  	if (flag == "active")
   24  		network.inUse = true;
   25  	else if (flag == "saved")
   26  		network.inUse = false;
   27  	else
   28  		return false;
   29  
   30  	network.name = name;
   31  	return true;
   32  }
   33  
   34  static std::string savedField(const std::string& field)
   35  {
   36  	std::string result;
   37  	for (size_t i = 0; i < field.size(); ++i)
   38  		if (field[i] == '\\' && i + 1 < field.size() && (field[i + 1] == '\\' || field[i + 1] == 't'))
   39  			result += field[++i] == 't' ? '\t' : '\\';
   40  		else result += field[i];
   41  	return result;
   42  }
   43  
   44  bool WifiText::parseSavedDetailsLine(const std::string& rawLine, SavedNetwork& network)
   45  {
   46  	const std::string line = withoutCR(rawLine);
   47  	const size_t first = line.find('\t'), last = line.rfind('\t');
   48  	if (first == std::string::npos || first == last || line.find('\t', first + 1) != last)
   49  		return false;
   50  	const auto flag = line.substr(last + 1);
   51  	if (flag != "active" && flag != "saved") return false;
   52  	network.name = savedField(line.substr(0, first));
   53  	network.ssid = savedField(line.substr(first + 1, last - first - 1));
   54  	network.inUse = flag == "active";
   55  	return !network.name.empty() && !network.ssid.empty();
   56  }
   57  
   58  std::vector<WifiText::SavedNetwork> WifiText::profilesForSsid(const std::string& ssid, const std::vector<SavedNetwork>& saved)
   59  {
   60  	std::vector<SavedNetwork> result;
   61  	for (const auto& profile : saved)
   62  		if ((profile.ssid.empty() ? profile.name : profile.ssid) == ssid)
   63  			result.push_back(profile);
   64  	return result;
   65  }
   66  
   67  std::string WifiText::preferredProfile(const std::string& ssid, const std::vector<SavedNetwork>& saved)
   68  {
   69  	const auto matching = profilesForSsid(ssid, saved);
   70  	if (matching.size() == 1) return matching.front().name;
   71  	int active = 0;
   72  	std::string name, namesake;
   73  	for (const auto& profile : matching)
   74  	{
   75  		if (profile.name == ssid) namesake = profile.name;
   76  		if (profile.inUse) { ++active; name = profile.name; }
   77  		if (profile.inUse && profile.name == ssid) return profile.name;
   78  	}
   79  	if (active == 1) return name;
   80  	return active == 0 ? namesake : "";
   81  }
   82  
   83  std::vector<WifiText::SavedNetwork> WifiText::parseSaved(const std::vector<std::string>& lines)
   84  {
   85  	std::vector<SavedNetwork> networks;
   86  	for (const auto& line : lines)
   87  	{
   88  		SavedNetwork network;
   89  		if (parseSavedLine(line, network))
   90  			networks.push_back(network);
   91  	}
   92  	return networks;
   93  }
   94  
   95  std::string WifiText::parseCurrent(const std::vector<std::string>& lines)
   96  {
   97  	for (const auto& line : lines)
   98  	{
   99  		const std::string ssid = withoutCR(line);
  100  		if (!ssid.empty())
  101  			return ssid;
  102  	}
  103  	return "";
  104  }
  105  
  106  std::vector<WifiText::PickerRow> WifiText::pickerRows(const std::vector<std::string>& inRange, const std::vector<SavedNetwork>& saved, const std::string& current,
  107  	bool savedKnown, bool currentKnown)
  108  {
  109  	// The device's network when `wifictl current` could not say: the saved
  110  	// list marks the profile that is active (#308 F-WF-03).
  111  	std::string joinedNow = current;
  112  	if (!currentKnown && savedKnown)
  113  		for (const auto& network : saved)
  114  			if (network.inUse)
  115  			{
  116  				joinedNow = network.ssid.empty() ? network.name : network.ssid;
  117  				break;
  118  			}
  119  
  120  	const bool detailed = [&saved] {
  121  		for (const auto& profile : saved) if (!profile.ssid.empty()) return true;
  122  		return false;
  123  	}();
  124  	auto isSaved = [&saved](const std::string& name) { return !profilesForSsid(name, saved).empty(); };
  125  
  126  	std::vector<PickerRow> rows;
  127  	auto listed = [&rows](const std::string& name)
  128  	{
  129  		for (const auto& row : rows)
  130  			if (row.name == name)
  131  				return true;
  132  		return false;
  133  	};
  134  
  135  	// The profile the device is on (#308 claude F-WF-12, gpt F-WF-03).
  136  	// wifictl speaks two names: current the SSID, saved and join a profile's
  137  	// name, and the two differ for a renamed profile or NetworkManager's
  138  	// "Home 1". So the connected row joins by a profile, in this order:
  139  	//   - the one named as its SSID, when that one is up;
  140  	//   - else the one profile that is up -- even beside a namesake that is
  141  	//     not: the name used to decide first, and an old "Home" left saved
  142  	//     beside the "Home 1" in use took the row's FORGET and JOIN (audit of
  143  	//     the fix round, gpt G2-E-app-01);
  144  	//   - else, with none up (the list's flags behind the join), the one
  145  	//     named as the SSID, as before.
  146  	// Two or more up and none of them named so (a second adapter: saved's
  147  	// ACTIVE is any adapter's, where current is this device's) is not
  148  	// guessed at, a namesake that is not up included -- it is the one
  149  	// profile known not to be this connection -- and the row joins by its
  150  	// name, as before.
  151  	std::string joinedProfile;
  152  	if (!joinedNow.empty() && savedKnown && !detailed)
  153  	{
  154  		int up = 0;
  155  		bool namedUp = false;
  156  		std::string theOneUp;
  157  		for (const auto& network : saved)
  158  			if (network.inUse)
  159  			{
  160  				up++;
  161  				theOneUp = network.name;
  162  				if (network.name == joinedNow)
  163  					namedUp = true;
  164  			}
  165  		if (namedUp)
  166  			joinedProfile = joinedNow;
  167  		else if (up == 1)
  168  			joinedProfile = theOneUp;
  169  		else if (up == 0 && isSaved(joinedNow))
  170  			joinedProfile = joinedNow;
  171  	}
  172  
  173  	if (detailed && savedKnown) joinedProfile = preferredProfile(joinedNow, saved);
  174  
  175  	if (!joinedNow.empty())
  176  	{
  177  		PickerRow row{ joinedNow, !joinedProfile.empty(), true, savedKnown };
  178  		row.profile = joinedProfile;
  179  		rows.push_back(row);
  180  	}
  181  
  182  	for (const auto& rawName : inRange)
  183  	{
  184  		const std::string name = withoutCR(rawName);
  185  		if (name.empty() || listed(name))
  186  			continue;
  187  		PickerRow row{ name, savedKnown && isSaved(name), false, savedKnown };
  188  		if (row.saved)
  189  			row.profile = preferredProfile(name, saved);
  190  		rows.push_back(row);
  191  	}
  192  	return rows;
  193  }
  194  
  195  bool WifiText::parseJoin(const std::vector<std::string>& lines)
  196  {
  197  	for (const auto& rawLine : lines)
  198  		if (withoutCR(rawLine) == "joined")
  199  			return true;
  200  	return false;
  201  }
  202  
  203  WifiText::JoinFailure WifiText::joinFailure(int exitCode)
  204  {
  205  	return exitCode == 2 ? JoinFailure::ServiceNotAnswering : JoinFailure::MayBeKey;
  206  }
  207  
  208  WifiText::ForgetOutcome WifiText::parseForget(const std::vector<std::string>& lines)
  209  {
  210  	ForgetOutcome outcome;
  211  	for (const auto& rawLine : lines)
  212  	{
  213  		const std::string line = withoutCR(rawLine);
  214  		if (line == "forgotten")
  215  			outcome.forgotten = true;
  216  		else if (line == "disconnected" && outcome.forgotten)
  217  			outcome.disconnected = true;
  218  	}
  219  	return outcome;
  220  }
  221  
  222  WifiText::PressAction WifiText::pressAction(const PickerRow& row)
  223  {
  224  	if (row.connected)
  225  		return PressAction::Join;
  226  	if (row.saved)
  227  		return PressAction::ChooseSaved;
  228  	if (!row.savedKnown)
  229  		return PressAction::CheckAgain;
  230  	return PressAction::AskKey;
  231  }
  232  
  233  WifiText::PressAction WifiText::manualAction(const std::string& name, const std::string& current, const std::vector<SavedNetwork>& saved, bool savedKnown)
  234  {
  235  	if (!name.empty() && name == current)
  236  		return PressAction::Join;
  237  	for (const auto& network : saved)
  238  		if ((network.ssid.empty() ? network.name : network.ssid) == name)
  239  			return PressAction::Join;
  240  	if (!savedKnown)
  241  		return PressAction::CheckAgain;
  242  	return PressAction::AskKey;
  243  }
  244  
  245  // A row joins by its profile when it has one, and by its name when not --
  246  // the connected row whose profile could not be told, which join then
  247  // answers for itself.
  248  std::string WifiText::joinName(const PickerRow& row)
  249  {
  250  	return row.profile.empty() ? row.name : row.profile;
  251  }
  252  
  253  // A typed name is taken as a row would be: the network the device is on
  254  // joins by its profile, anything else by the name as typed.
  255  std::string WifiText::manualJoinName(const std::string& name, const std::string& current, const std::string& currentProfile)
  256  {
  257  	if (!name.empty() && name == current && !currentProfile.empty())
  258  		return currentProfile;
  259  	return name;
  260  }
  261  
  262  std::string WifiText::joinedNotice(const std::string& name, const std::string& connectedWord)
  263  {
  264  	return name + " : " + connectedWord;
  265  }
```

```text
    1  #pragma once
    2  #ifndef ES_APP_WIFI_TEXT_H
    3  #define ES_APP_WIFI_TEXT_H
    4  
    5  // The pure text of the Wi-Fi rows (fork #191): what wifictl prints for the
    6  // interface, read into values the pages can show, with nothing from the
    7  // window, the settings or a script behind it, so es-unit-tests can hold the
    8  // rules (es-app/tests/unit). The thin shells that run wifictl are in
    9  // ApiSystem; the rows themselves are in GuiMenu.
   10  //
   11  // The setting wifi.ssid is the network the player configured last.
   12  // NetworkManager keeps a profile for every network the device has joined and
   13  // autoconnects to whichever remembered one is in range, so what the device is
   14  // on and what the setting says are two different facts; these are the shapes
   15  // wifictl reports the first in.
   16  
   17  #include <string>
   18  #include <vector>
   19  
   20  namespace WifiText
   21  {
   22  	// A network NetworkManager remembers, as one line of `wifictl saved`
   23  	// reads: <name><TAB>active or <name><TAB>saved.
   24  	struct SavedNetwork
   25  	{
   26  		std::string name;
   27  		bool inUse = false;
   28  		std::string ssid; // optional for legacy two-column readers
   29  	};
   30  
   31  	// One line of `wifictl saved`. False for a line that is not a network:
   32  	// no tab, an empty name, a flag that is neither word. A trailing CR is
   33  	// tolerated. The name keeps its case and every space in it -- a network's
   34  	// name is case-sensitive, and is what the player recognises it by -- and
   35  	// the flag is read after the last tab, so a tab inside a name still
   36  	// leaves the flag whole.
   37  	bool parseSavedLine(const std::string& line, SavedNetwork& network);
   38  	// Current wifictl saved --ssid: escaped profile, escaped SSID, state.
   39  	bool parseSavedDetailsLine(const std::string& line, SavedNetwork& network);
   40  	std::vector<SavedNetwork> profilesForSsid(const std::string& ssid, const std::vector<SavedNetwork>& saved);
   41  	std::string preferredProfile(const std::string& ssid, const std::vector<SavedNetwork>& saved);
   42  
   43  
   44  	// Every network in the lines, in the order given (nmcli puts the one in
   45  	// use first); lines that are not networks are passed over.
   46  	std::vector<SavedNetwork> parseSaved(const std::vector<std::string>& lines);
   47  
   48  	// The first line of `wifictl current`, the SSID the device is joined to;
   49  	// empty when there is none. Only a trailing CR is trimmed: a name may
   50  	// begin or end with a space.
   51  	std::string parseCurrent(const std::vector<std::string>& lines);
   52  
   53  	// A row of the Wi-Fi picker (fork #191; the maintainer's paradigm of
   54  	// 2026-09-15: the WI-FI NETWORK row -- WI-FI SSID until D-UI-071 renamed
   55  	// it for its new value -- is the network the device is on, the
   56  	// list behind it is what is in range, and a saved one joins with a
   57  	// press). Built by pickerRows from what `wifictl list` found, the
   58  	// profiles `wifictl saved` holds and the network `wifictl current`
   59  	// answered.
   60  	struct PickerRow
   61  	{
   62  		std::string name;
   63  		bool saved = false;
   64  		bool connected = false;
   65  		// Whether `saved` is an answer: false when `wifictl saved` could
   66  		// not be asked (#308 F-WF-03/06), and the row is then neither saved
   67  		// nor unsaved, only unknown.
   68  		bool savedKnown = true;
   69  		// The saved profile a press joins by, as `wifictl join` takes it;
   70  		// empty when the row has none known.
   71  		std::string profile;
   72  	};
   73  
   74  	// The rows in the order the list shows them: the network joined now
   75  	// first (added when the scan missed it -- a hidden network is joined and
   76  	// not listed), then the rest in the scan's order, empty names and repeats
   77  	// dropped, each marked saved when NetworkManager holds a profile for it.
   78  	// Saved networks out of range are not rows: this list is what can be
   79  	// joined from here; MANAGE SAVED NETWORKS lists them all. Names compare
   80  	// exactly, as NetworkManager does.
   81  	// savedKnown and currentKnown say whether `wifictl saved` and `wifictl
   82  	// current` answered (ApiSystem's two getters return it): an empty list
   83  	// and no list are different answers (#308 F-WF-03/06).
   84  	std::vector<PickerRow> pickerRows(const std::vector<std::string>& inRange, const std::vector<SavedNetwork>& saved, const std::string& current,
   85  		bool savedKnown = true, bool currentKnown = true);
   86  
   87  	// What a press on a row does, and what INPUT MANUALLY does with a name.
   88  	//
   89  	// The network the device is on is joined too, not taken on trust
   90  	// (#308 F-WF-05): the row was built from a snapshot, and the device may
   91  	// have dropped it or moved to another since. wifictl join answers
   92  	// "joined" at once for a profile that is active and brings back one that
   93  	// is not, so the press confirms or repairs, and the picker closes only
   94  	// on its answer. A network whose profile could not be asked about is
   95  	// asked about again, never taken for one with no profile: the key path
   96  	// (wifictl connect) deletes and rebuilds a profile of that name (#308
   97  	// F-WF-03/06).
   98  	//
   99  	// A saved network the device is not on is a choice, not a join (#318,
  100  	// D-UI-118, taken from ROCKNIX's own saved-Wi-Fi work): CONNECT with the
  101  	// key NetworkManager holds, or FORGET it. The connected row still joins
  102  	// at once -- the press confirms or repairs -- and a typed name that is
  103  	// saved still joins, since typing it is asking to join it.
  104  	enum class PressAction
  105  	{
  106  		Join,         // join by the profile NetworkManager holds (wifictl join)
  107  		ChooseSaved,  // a saved network not in use: CONNECT / FORGET / CANCEL
  108  		AskKey,       // a network with no profile: its key, then connect
  109  		CheckAgain    // whether it has a profile could not be asked: never assume it has none
  110  	};
  111  	PressAction pressAction(const PickerRow& row);
  112  	PressAction manualAction(const std::string& name, const std::string& current, const std::vector<SavedNetwork>& saved, bool savedKnown);
  113  
  114  	// The name `wifictl join` is handed for a row's press, and for a typed
  115  	// name (current is the connected row's name, currentProfile its
  116  	// profile).
  117  	std::string joinName(const PickerRow& row);
  118  	std::string manualJoinName(const std::string& name, const std::string& current, const std::string& currentProfile);
  119  
  120  	// The toast when the picker has joined a network, in the toast's shape
  121  	// (es-ui-style-guide.md: <glyph> <subject> : <outcome>): the name as
  122  	// NetworkManager has it, then the word the row uses for the same fact
  123  	// (#308 F-WF-08). The caller puts the glyph in front.
  124  	std::string joinedNotice(const std::string& name, const std::string& connectedWord);
  125  
  126  	// What `wifictl join` printed: "joined" once the saved network's profile
  127  	// is active. Anything else, or nothing, is not a join that happened.
  128  	bool parseJoin(const std::vector<std::string>& lines);
  129  
  130  	// What a join that did not happen means, from `wifictl join`'s exit code
  131  	// (#308 2-wifi claude F-WF-03, gpt F-WF-06): 2 is NetworkManager not
  132  	// answering -- nothing was tried, and the key is not in question; any
  133  	// other (1: the profile would not come up or is not saved, 124: the
  134  	// timeout's bound on the activation, 0 without "joined") may be the key.
  135  	enum class JoinFailure { ServiceNotAnswering, MayBeKey };
  136  	JoinFailure joinFailure(int exitCode);
  137  
  138  	// wifictl join's answer, as ApiSystem::joinWifiNetwork gives it: the
  139  	// exit code (0 joined; 1 not joined; 2 NetworkManager not answering;
  140  	// 124 the timeout's bound), in a type that does not turn into a bool.
  141  	// The call returned bool, true for joined, until #308 F-WF-03/06, and 0
  142  	// means joined now: a caller left testing it as a truth value would
  143  	// compile and read every join backwards (audit of the fixes, E2 claude
  144  	// G-E2-01). This one does not compile.
  145  	struct JoinAnswer
  146  	{
  147  		int code = 1;
  148  		bool joined() const { return code == 0; }
  149  	};
  150  
  151  	// What `wifictl forget` printed: "forgotten" when the profile went, then
  152  	// "disconnected" on a second line when it was the one in use. A
  153  	// "disconnected" with no "forgotten" before it is not a forget that
  154  	// happened; nothing printed is not one either -- a caller reads the
  155  	// word, not the absence of an error.
  156  	struct ForgetOutcome
  157  	{
  158  		bool forgotten = false;
  159  		bool disconnected = false;
  160  	};
  161  	ForgetOutcome parseForget(const std::vector<std::string>& lines);
  162  }
  163  
  164  #endif // ES_APP_WIFI_TEXT_H
```
