```text
   35  	mgr->RegisterComponent(mNetworkStateWatcher);
   36  
   37  	if (ApiSystem::getInstance()->isScriptingSupported(ApiSystem::UPGRADE))
   38  		mgr->RegisterComponent(&mCheckUpdatesComponent);
   39  
   40  	if (ApiSystem::getInstance()->isScriptingSupported(ApiSystem::PADSINFO))
   41  		mgr->RegisterComponent(&mCheckPadsBatteryLevelComponent);
   42  
   43  	mgr->RegisterNotify(this);
   44  
   45  	LOG(LogDebug) << "NetworkThread : Starting";
   46  	InputManager::joystickChanged += this;
   47  }
   48  
   49  CheckPadsBatteryLevelComponent::CheckPadsBatteryLevelComponent()
   50  {
   51  #if WIN32		
   52  	mEnabled = false; // Windows uses SDL_JOYBATTERYUPDATED event instead
   53  #else
   54  	mEnabled = ApiSystem::getInstance()->isScriptingSupported(ApiSystem::PADSINFO);
   55  #endif
   56  }
   57  
   58  bool CheckPadsBatteryLevelComponent::check()
   59  {
   60  	bool changed = false;
   61  
   62  	auto padsInfo = ApiSystem::getInstance()->getPadsInfo();
   63  
   64  	if (mPadsInfo.size() != padsInfo.size())
   65  		changed = true;
   66  	else
   67  	{
   68  		for (int i = 0; i < padsInfo.size(); i++)
   69  		{
   70  			if (padsInfo[i].status == "Charging")
   71  				padsInfo[i].battery = -1;
   72  
   73  			if (mPadsInfo[i].battery != padsInfo[i].battery || mPadsInfo[i].status != padsInfo[i].status || mPadsInfo[i].id != padsInfo[i].id || mPadsInfo[i].name != padsInfo[i].name || mPadsInfo[i].path != padsInfo[i].path || mPadsInfo[i].device != padsInfo[i].device)
   74  			{
   75  				changed = true;
   76  				break;
   77  			}
   78  		}
   79  	}
   80  
   81  	mPadsInfo = padsInfo;
   82  	return changed;
   83  }
   84  
   85  bool CheckUpdatesComponent::enabled()
   86  {
   87  	return mEnabled && SystemConf::getInstance()->getBool("updates.enabled") && ApiSystem::getInstance()->isScriptingSupported(ApiSystem::UPGRADE);
   88  }
   89  
   90  bool CheckUpdatesComponent::check()
   91  {
   92  	LOG(LogDebug) << "CheckUpdatesComponent : Checking for updates";
   93  
   94  	std::vector<std::string> msgtbl;
   95  	if (ApiSystem::getInstance()->canUpdate(msgtbl))
   96  	{
   97  		std::string msg = "";
   98  		for (int i = 0; i < msgtbl.size(); i++)
   99  		{
  100  			if (i != 0) msg += "\n";
  101  			msg += msgtbl[i];
  102  		}
  103  
  104  		LOG(LogDebug) << "CheckUpdatesComponent : Update available " << msg.c_str();
  105  		mLastUpdateMessage = msg;
  106  		mEnabled = false;
  107  		return true;
  108  	}
  109  	else
  110  		LOG(LogDebug) << "NetworkThread : No update found";
  111  
  112  	return false;
  113  }
  114  
  115  bool CheckCheevosTokenComponent::enabled()
  116  {
  117  	return SystemConf::getInstance()->getBool("global.retroachievements");
  118  };
  119  
  120  bool CheckCheevosTokenComponent::check()
  121  {
  122  	// The regular schedule unless this check decides otherwise below, so a
  123  	// check that returns early -- the switch off, no account -- never
  124  	// inherits a short retry from the one before it.
  125  	mNextDelayMs = CheevosRetry::ScheduledMs;
  126  
  127  	if (!enabled())
  128  		return false;
  129  
  130  	std::string cheevosUsername = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("global.retroachievements.username");
  131  	std::string cheevosPassword = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("global.retroachievements.password");
  132  	
  133  	if (cheevosUsername.empty() || cheevosPassword.empty())
  134  		return false;
  135  
  136  	std::string tokenOrError;
  137  	bool refused = false;
  138  	if (RetroAchievements::testAccount(cheevosUsername, cheevosPassword, tokenOrError, &refused))
  139  	{
  140  		mRetryWhenOnline = false;
  141  		mUnreachableInARow = 0;
  142  		if (tokenOrError == SystemConf::getInstance()->get("global.retroachievements.token"))
  143  		{
  144  			LOG(LogInfo) << "[CheckCheevosTokenComponent] Cheevos token is unchanged.";
  145  		}
  146  		else
  147  		{
  148  			LOG(LogInfo) << "[CheckCheevosTokenComponent] Generated a new cheevos token, saving.";
  149  			mLastToken = tokenOrError;
  150  			return true;
  151  		}
  152  	}
  153  	else
  154  	{
  155  		// A refusal is the account's to fix and no network changes it; any
  156  		// other failure is the first check of a boot running before the
  157  		// link was up (#175), and NetworkThread asks for it again when
  158  		// the network arrives.
  159  		mRetryWhenOnline = !refused;
  160  		mUnreachableInARow = refused ? 0 : mUnreachableInARow + 1;
  161  		LOG(LogError) << "[CheckCheevosTokenComponent] Failed to generate a new cheevos token:*** " << tokenOrError;
  162  
  163  		// That network-up check can itself run before the resolver answers
  164  		// (RC-3 on #175: "Could not resolve hostname" five seconds after
  165  		// the address), and the watcher only speaks when the link changes.
  166  		// So while the link is up, try again shortly, a bounded number of
  167  		// times; WatchersManager reads updateTime() right after this.
  168  		mNextDelayMs = CheevosRetry::nextDelayMs(mRetryWhenOnline, mOnline, mUnreachableInARow);
  169  		if (mNextDelayMs != CheevosRetry::ScheduledMs)
  170  		{
  171  			LOG(LogWarning) << "[CheckCheevosTokenComponent] The network is up, so trying again in " << (mNextDelayMs / 1000) << " s (" << mUnreachableInARow << " of " << CheevosRetry::Attempts << ")";
  172  		}
  173  	}
  174  
  175  	return false;
  176  }
  177  
  178  void CheckCheevosTokenComponent::setOnline(bool online)
  179  {
  180  	// A link that has just come up opens a new window: the failures before
  181  	// it were the old link's, or no link's at all.
  182  	if (online && !mOnline)
  183  		mUnreachableInARow = 0;
  184  
  185  	mOnline = online;
  186  }
  187  
  188  
  189  void NetworkThread::onJoystickChanged()
  190  {
  191  	WatchersManager::getInstance()->ResetComponent(&mCheckPadsBatteryLevelComponent);
  192  }
  193  
  194  void NetworkThread::OnWatcherChanged(IWatcher* component)
  195  {
  196  	if (component == &mCheckUpdatesComponent)
  197  	{
  198  		mWindow->displayNotificationMessage(_U("\uF019  ") + _("UPDATE AVAILABLE") + std::string(": ") + mCheckUpdatesComponent.getLastUpdateMessage());
  199  		return;
  200  	}
  201  
  202  	if (component == &mCheckPadsBatteryLevelComponent)
  203  	{
  204  		auto pads = mCheckPadsBatteryLevelComponent.getPadsInfo();
  205  
  206  		mWindow->postToUiThread([pads]() { for (auto pad : pads) InputManager::getInstance()->updateBatteryLevel(pad.id, pad.device, pad.path, pad.battery); });
  207  		return;
  208  	}
  209  
  210  	if (component == mNetworkStateWatcher)
  211  	{
  212  		// The first sign-in of a boot can run before the network is up and
  213  		// fail (#175); the next scheduled one is two hours off, and the
  214  		// launch scripts read the token it would have written. So when the
  215  		// network arrives, ask for that check now. Posted to the interface
  216  		// thread: this runs on the watchers' thread, under the lock that
  217  		// ResetComponent takes. The component hears about the link first,
  218  		// on this same thread, so the check that follows knows it is worth
  219  		// a short retry if the resolver is not answering yet.
  220  		bool online = mNetworkStateWatcher->isConnected();
  221  		mCheckCheevosTokenComponent.setOnline(online);
  222  
  223  		// The device has come online (fork #292, #293, #305; D-UI-095,
  224  		// D-RA-030, D-UI-109): the exit sync that was skipped for no network
  225  		// runs first, with its card; then the RetroAchievements batch -- the
  226  		// awards the proxy held while offline go up with a card that follows
  227  		// the queue, and the recently played games' achievement data is
  228  		// cached so they earn offline too (the ctl's top-up, fork #179, with
  229  		// a card once it has work). With no saves owed the batch runs at
  230  		// once. The ctl decides whether the toggle is on, an account is
  231  		// signed in and RetroAchievements answers, and bounds how often it
  232  		// runs. Posted to the interface thread: this runs on the watchers'
  233  		// thread.
  234  		if (online)
  235  		{
  236  			Window* window = mWindow;
  237  			mWindow->postToUiThread([window]() { ProxyCards::linkReturned(window); });
  238  		}
  239  
  240  		// The startup index asks RetroAchievements for its hash library as the
  241  		// interface starts, and a handheld's Wi-Fi is often still associating
  242  		// then: the library does not come, and the run ends with nothing
  243  		// indexed. When the link arrives, run it again; once the library has
  244  		// come this session there is nothing to repeat (fork #183). D-RA-013's
  245  		// promise -- a game added later is cached when the device is next
  246  		// connected -- rests on the index knowing the game. On the interface
  247  		// thread, as every hasher start is; the netplay index is left alone.
  248  		if (online && Settings::CheevosCheckIndexesAtStart() && !ThreadedHasher::cheevosLibraryCameThisSession())
  249  		{
  250  			Window* window = mWindow;
  251  			mWindow->postToUiThread([window]() { SystemData::startIndexesAtStart(window, true); });
  252  		}
  253  
  254  		if (online && mCheckCheevosTokenComponent.retryWhenOnline())
  255  		{
  256  			CheckCheevosTokenComponent* cheevos = &mCheckCheevosTokenComponent;
  257  			mWindow->postToUiThread([cheevos]() { WatchersManager::getInstance()->ResetComponent(cheevos); });
  258  		}
  259  		return;
  260  	}
  261  
  262  	if (component == &mCheckCheevosTokenComponent)
  263  	{
  264  		auto token = mCheckCheevosTokenComponent.getLastToken();
  265  
  266  		mWindow->postToUiThread([token]() 
  267  			{ 
  268  				SystemConf::getInstance()->set("global.retroachievements.token", token);
  269  				SystemConf::getInstance()->saveSystemConf();
  270  			});
  271  
  272  		return;
  273  	}
  274  }
```

```text
   49  class CheckCheevosTokenComponent : public IWatcher
   50  {
   51  public:
   52  	std::string& getLastToken() { return mLastToken; }
   53  
   54  	// The last check could not reach RetroAchievements, as against being
   55  	// turned down by it: worth running again as soon as the network is up
   56  	// (#175), rather than at the next scheduled check two hours on.
   57  	bool retryWhenOnline() const { return mRetryWhenOnline; }
   58  
   59  	// NetworkThread's view of the link, told on the watchers' thread -- the
   60  	// one check() runs on -- whenever it changes. A link that has just come
   61  	// up opens a new window of short retries (#175, CheevosRetry.h).
   62  	void setOnline(bool online);
   63  
   64  protected:
   65  	bool enabled() override;
   66  	// Decided by the last check(): CheevosRetry::ScheduledMs (120 minutes)
   67  	// unless that check could not reach the server while the network was
   68  	// up, when it is a short retry, a bounded number of times.
   69  	int  updateTime() override { return mNextDelayMs; }
   70  	int  initialUpdateTime() override { return 100; } // 100ms
   71  	bool check() override;
   72  
   73  private:
   74  	std::string mLastToken;
   75  	bool mRetryWhenOnline = false;
   76  	bool mOnline = false;
   77  	int  mUnreachableInARow = 0;
   78  	int  mNextDelayMs = CheevosRetry::ScheduledMs;
   79  };
```

```text
 1558  void CollectionSystemManager::reloadCollection(const std::string& collectionName, bool repopulateGamelist)
 1559  {
 1560  	auto autoc = mAutoCollectionSystemsData.find(collectionName);
 1561  	if (autoc != mAutoCollectionSystemsData.cend())
 1562  	{
 1563  		if (repopulateGamelist)
 1564  			repopulateGameListView(collectionName);
 1565  
 1566  		return;
 1567  	}
 1568  	
 1569  	auto data = mCustomCollectionSystemsData.find(collectionName);
 1570  	if (data == mCustomCollectionSystemsData.cend())
 1571  		return;
 1572  
 1573  	if (data->second.filteredIndex != nullptr)
 1574  	{
 1575  		data->second.isPopulated = false;
 1576  		populateCustomCollection(&data->second);
 1577  
 1578  		if (!repopulateGamelist)
 1579  			return;
 1580  
 1581  		auto bundle = CollectionSystemManager::get()->getCustomCollectionsBundle();
 1582  		for (auto ff : bundle->getRootFolder()->getChildren())
 1583  		{
 1584  			if (ff->getType() == FOLDER && ff->getName() == collectionName)
 1585  			{
 1586  				auto view = ViewController::get()->getGameListView(bundle, false);
 1587  				if (view != nullptr)
 1588  				{
 1589  					ViewController::get()->reloadGameListView(bundle);
 1590  
 1591  					ISimpleGameListView* sgview = dynamic_cast<ISimpleGameListView*>(view.get());
 1592  					if (sgview != nullptr)
 1593  						sgview->moveToFolder((FolderData*)ff);
 1594  				}
 1595  
 1596  				return;
 1597  			}
 1598  		}
 1599  	}
 1600  	
 1601  	if (repopulateGamelist)
 1602  		repopulateGameListView(collectionName);
 1603  }
```

```text
   74  	//returns if file1 should come before file2
   75  	bool compareName(const FileData* file1, const FileData* file2)
   76  	{
   77  		// we compare the actual metadata name, as collection files have the system appended which messes up the order
   78  		const std::string& name1 = file1->getSortName();
   79  		const std::string& name2 = file2->getSortName();
   80  
   81  		if (Settings::IgnoreLeadingArticles())
   82  		{
   83  			static auto articles = Utils::String::commaStringToVector(_("A,AN,THE"));
   84  			auto name1a = stripLeadingArticle(name1, articles);
   85  			auto name2a = stripLeadingArticle(name2, articles);
   86  
   87  			return Utils::String::compareIgnoreCase(name1a, name2a) < 0;
   88  		}
   89  
   90  		return Utils::String::compareIgnoreCase(name1, name2) < 0;
   91  	}
   92  
   93  	std::string stripLeadingArticle(const std::string &string, const std::vector<std::string> &articles)
   94  	{
   95  		const auto candidate = Utils::String::trim(string);
   96  		const auto index = candidate.find_first_of(" \t\r\n");
   97  		if (index == std::string::npos)
   98  		{
   99  			return string;
  100  		}
  101  		const auto maybeArticle = candidate.substr(0, index);
  102  		for (auto &article: articles)
  103  		{
  104  			if (Utils::String::compareIgnoreCase(article, maybeArticle) == 0)
  105  			{
  106  				return Utils::String::trim(candidate.substr(article.length()));
  107  			}
  108  		}
  109  		return string;
  110  	}
  111
```

```text
  255  bool addFileDataNode(pugi::xml_node& parent, FileData* file, const char* tag, SystemData* system, bool fullPaths = false)
  256  {

  260  	//write metadata
  261  	file->getMetadata().appendToXML(newNode, true, system->getStartPath(), fullPaths);
  262  
  263  	if(newNode.children().begin() == newNode.child("name") //first element is name
  264  		&& ++newNode.children().begin() == newNode.children().end() //theres only one element
  265  		&& newNode.child("name").text().get() == file->getDisplayName()) //the name is the default
  266  	{
  267  		//if the only info is the default name, don't bother with this node
  268  		//delete it and ultimately do nothing
  269  		parent.remove_child(newNode);
```

```text
  288  bool saveToXml(FileData* file, const std::string& fileName, bool fullPaths)

  291  	if (system == nullptr)
  292  		return false;

  299  	root.append_attribute("parentHash").set_value(system->getGamelistHash());
  300  
  301  	if (addFileDataNode(root, file, tag, system, fullPaths))
  302  	{
  303  		std::string folder = Utils::FileSystem::getParent(fileName);
  304  		if (!Utils::FileSystem::exists(folder))
  305  			Utils::FileSystem::createDirectory(folder);
  306  
  307  		Utils::FileSystem::removeFile(fileName);
  308  		if (!doc.save_file(WINSTRINGW(fileName).c_str()))
  309  		{
  310  			LOG(LogError) << "Error saving metadata to \"" << fileName << "\" (for system " << system->getName() << ")!";
  311  			return false;
  312  		}
  313  
  314  		return true;
  315  	}
```

```text
  374  void updateGamelist(SystemData* system)
  375  {
  376  	// We do this by reading the XML again, adding changes and then writing it back,
  377  	// because there might be information missing in our systemdata which would then miss in the new XML.
  378  	// We have the complete information for every game though, so we can simply remove a game
  379  	// we already have in the system from the XML, and then add it back from its GameData information...
  380  
  381  	if (system == nullptr || Settings::IgnoreGamelist())
  382  		return;
  383  
  384  	if (!system->isGameSystem() || system->isCollection() || (!Settings::HiddenSystemsShowGames() && system->isHidden()))
  385  		return;
  386  
  387  	FolderData* rootFolder = system->getRootFolder();
  388  	if (rootFolder == nullptr)
  389  	{
  390  		LOG(LogError) << "Found no root folder for system \"" << system->getName() << "\"!";
  391  		return;
  392  	}
  393  
  394  	std::vector<FileData*> dirtyFiles;
  395  	
  396  	auto files = rootFolder->getFilesRecursive(GAME | FOLDER, false, nullptr, false);
  397  	for (auto file : files)
  398  		if (file->getSystem() == system && file->getMetadata().wasChanged())
  399  			dirtyFiles.push_back(file);
  400  
  401  	if (dirtyFiles.size() == 0)
  402  	{
  403  		clearTemporaryGamelistRecovery(system);
  404  		return;
  405  	}
  406  
  407  	int numUpdated = 0;
  408  
  409  	pugi::xml_document doc;
  410  	pugi::xml_node root;
  411  	std::string xmlReadPath = system->getGamelistPath(false);
  412  
  413  	if(Utils::FileSystem::exists(xmlReadPath))
  414  	{
  415  		//parse an existing file first
  416  		pugi::xml_parse_result result = doc.load_file(WINSTRINGW(xmlReadPath).c_str());
  417  		if(!result)
  418  			LOG(LogError) << "Error parsing XML file \"" << xmlReadPath << "\"!\n	" << result.description();
  419  
  420  		root = doc.child("gameList");
  421  		if(!root)
  422  		{
  423  			LOG(LogError) << "Could not find <gameList> node in gamelist \"" << xmlReadPath << "\"!";
  424  			root = doc.append_child("gameList");
  425  		}
  426  	}
  427  	else //set up an empty gamelist to append to		
  428  		root = doc.append_child("gameList");
  429  
  430  	std::map<std::string, pugi::xml_node> xmlMap;
  431  
  432  	for (pugi::xml_node fileNode : root.children())
  433  	{
  434  		pugi::xml_node path = fileNode.child("path");
  435  		if (path)
  436  		{
  437  			std::string nodePath = Utils::FileSystem::getCanonicalPath(Utils::FileSystem::resolveRelativePath(path.text().get(), system->getStartPath(), true));
  438  			xmlMap[nodePath] = fileNode;
  439  		}
  440  	}
  441  	
  442  	// iterate through all files, checking if they're already in the XML
  443  	for(auto file : dirtyFiles)
  444  	{
  445  		bool removed = false;
  446  
  447  		// check if the file already exists in the XML
  448  		// if it does, remove it before adding
  449  		auto xmf = xmlMap.find(Utils::FileSystem::getCanonicalPath(file->getPath()));
  450  		if (xmf != xmlMap.cend())
  451  		{
  452  			removed = true;
  453  			root.remove_child(xmf->second);
  454  		}
  455  		
  456  		const char* tag = (file->getType() == GAME) ? "game" : "folder";
  457  
  458  		// it was either removed or never existed to begin with; either way, we can add it now
  459  		if (addFileDataNode(root, file, tag, system))
  460  			++numUpdated; // Only if really added
  461  		else if (removed)
  462  			++numUpdated; // Only if really removed
  463  	}
  464  
  465  	// Now write the file
  466  	if (numUpdated > 0) 
  467  	{
  468  		//make sure the folders leading up to this path exist (or the write will fail)
  469  		std::string xmlWritePath(system->getGamelistPath(true));
  470  		Utils::FileSystem::createDirectory(Utils::FileSystem::getParent(xmlWritePath));
  471  
  472  		LOG(LogInfo) << "Added/Updated " << numUpdated << " entities in '" << xmlReadPath << "'";
  473  
  474  		if (!doc.save_file(WINSTRINGW(xmlWritePath).c_str()))
  475  			LOG(LogError) << "Error saving gamelist.xml to \"" << xmlWritePath << "\" (for system " << system->getName() << ")!";
  476  		else
  477  			clearTemporaryGamelistRecovery(system);
  478  	}
  479  	else
  480  		clearTemporaryGamelistRecovery(system);
  481  }
```

```text
  328  void KeyMappingFile::save(const std::string& fileName)
  329  {
  330  	rapidjson::StringBuffer s;
  331  	rapidjson::PrettyWriter<rapidjson::StringBuffer> writer(s);
  332  
  333  	writer.StartObject();
  334  
  335  	int idx = 1;
  336  	for (auto player : players)
  337  	{
  338  		if (player.mappings.size() == 0)
  339  		{
  340  			idx++;
  341  			continue;
  342  		}
  343  		
  344  		writer.Key(("actions_player" + std::to_string(idx)).c_str());
  345  		writer.StartArray();
  346  
  347  		for (int m = 0 ; m < player.mappings.size() ; m++)		
  348  		{
  349  			auto mapping = player.mappings[m];
  350  
  351  			if (mapping.triggers.size() == 0 || (mapping.targets.size() == 0 && mapping.type != "mouse"))
  352  				continue;
  353  
  354  			writer.StartObject();
  355  			
  356  			writer.Key("trigger");
  357  
  358  			if (mapping.triggers.size() == 1)
  359  				writer.String((*mapping.triggers.begin()).c_str());
  360  			else
  361  			{
  362  				writer.StartArray();
  363  				for (auto v : mapping.triggers)
  364  					writer.String(v.c_str());
  365  
  366  				writer.EndArray();
  367  			}
  368  
  369  			if (!mapping.mode.empty())
  370  			{
  371  				writer.Key("mode");
  372  				writer.String(mapping.mode.c_str());
  373  			}
  374  
  375  			if (!mapping.type.empty())
  376  			{
  377  				writer.Key("type");
  378  				writer.String(mapping.type.c_str());
  379  			}
  380  
  381  			if (mapping.targets.size() > 0)
  382  			{
  383  				writer.Key("target");
  384  
  385  				if (mapping.targets.size() == 1)
  386  					writer.String((*mapping.targets.begin()).c_str());
  387  				else
  388  				{
  389  					writer.StartArray();
  390  
  391  					for (auto modifier : targetOrders)
  392  						if (mapping.targets.find(modifier) != mapping.targets.cend())
  393  							writer.String(modifier.c_str());
  394  
  395  					for (auto v : mapping.targets)
  396  						if (std::find(targetOrders.cbegin(), targetOrders.cend(), v) == targetOrders.cend())
  397  							writer.String(v.c_str());
  398
```

```text
   20  void Zaparoo::checkZaparooEnabledAsync(const std::function<void(bool enabled)>& func)
   21  {
   22  	std::thread([func]()
   23  	{
   24  		bool enabled = isZaparooEnabled(3000);
   25  		if (func)
   26  			func(enabled);
   27  	}).detach();

   30  bool Zaparoo::isZaparooEnabled(long defaultDelay)
   31  {
   32  	std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mZaparooLock);
   33  
   34  	if (mChecked)
   35  		return mAvailable;
   36  
   37  	HttpReqOptions options;
   38  	options.connectTimeout = defaultDelay;
   39  	HttpReq req("http://127.0.0.1:7497/health", &options);
   40  
   41  	if (req.wait())
   42  	{
   43  		std::string data = req.getContent();
   44  		mAvailable = (data.compare("OK") == 0);
   45  	}
   46  	else
   47  		mAvailable = false;
```

```text
  195  /// This class is used to call Scraper->search() in a thread. 
  196  /// This avoids freezes while building requests, as they can have some blocking code ( build rom md5, ask server for a token... )
  197  class ScraperAsyncRequestsBuilder : public ScraperSearchHandle
  198  {
  199  public:
  200  	ScraperAsyncRequestsBuilder(Scraper* scraper, ScraperSearch* ss)
  201  	{
  202  		mScraper = scraper;		
  203  		mSearch = ss;
  204  
  205  		mFinished = false;
  206  		mThread = new std::thread(&ScraperAsyncRequestsBuilder::loadSearchHandleAsync, this);
  207  	}
  208  	
  209  	void loadSearchHandleAsync()
  210  	{
  211  		try
  212  		{
  213  			mSearchHandle = mScraper->search(mSearch->params);
  214  		}
  215  		catch (std::exception& e)
  216  		{ 
  217  			mError = e.what();
  218  		}
  219  
  220  		mFinished = true;
  221  	}
  222  
  223  	virtual void update()
  224  	{
  225  		if (!mFinished)
  226  			return;
  227  
  228  		mFinished = false;
  229  
  230  		if (mThread && mThread->joinable())
  231  		{
  232  			mThread->join();
  233  			mThread = nullptr;
  234  		}
  235  
  236  		if (mSearchHandle == nullptr)
  237  			setError(mError);
  238  		else
  239  			setStatus(ASYNC_DONE);
  240  	}
  241  
  242  	ScraperSearchHandle* ExtractSearchHandle()
  243  	{
  244  		if (mSearchHandle)
  245  			return mSearchHandle.release();
  246  
  247  		return nullptr;
  248  	}
  249  
  250  private:
  251  	Scraper*		mScraper;
  252  	ScraperSearch*	mSearch;
  253  	std::thread*	mThread;
  254  
  255  	std::string		mError;
  256  	bool			mFinished;
  257  
  258  	std::unique_ptr<ScraperSearchHandle> mSearchHandle;
  259  };

  265
```

```text
  490  bool ViewController::checkLaunchOptions(FileData* game, LaunchGameOptions options, Vector3f center)
  491  {
  492  #ifdef _RPI_
  493  	if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("VideoOmxPlayer") && mCurrentView)
  494  		mCurrentView->onHide();
  495  #endif
  496  
  497  	if (!game->isExtensionCompatible())
  498  	{
  499  		auto gui = new GuiMsgBox(mWindow, _("WARNING: THE EMULATOR/CORE CURRENTLY SET DOES NOT SUPPORT THIS GAME'S FILE FORMAT.\nDO YOU WANT TO LAUNCH IT ANYWAY?"),
  500  			_("YES"), [this, game, options, center] { launch(game, options, center, false); },
  501  			_("NO"), nullptr, ICON_ERROR);
  502  
  503  		mWindow->pushGui(gui);
  504  		return false;
  505  	}
  506  
  507  	if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("CheckBiosesAtLaunch") && ApiSystem::getInstance()->isScriptingSupported(ApiSystem::ScriptId::BIOSINFORMATION))
  508  	{
  509  		auto bios = ApiSystem::getInstance()->getBiosInformations(game->getSourceFileData()->getSystem()->getName());
  510  		if (bios.size() != 0)
  511  		{
  512  			auto systemName = game->getSystem()->getName();
  513  			auto it = std::find_if(bios.cbegin(), bios.cend(), [&systemName](const BiosSystem& x) { return x.name == systemName; });
  514  			if (it != bios.cend() && it->bios.size() > 0)
  515  			{
  516  				bool hasMissing = std::find_if(it->bios.cbegin(), it->bios.cend(), [&systemName](const BiosFile& x) { return x.status == "MISSING"; }) != it->bios.cend();
  517  				if (hasMissing)
  518  				{
  519  					auto gui = new GuiMsgBox(mWindow, _("WARNING: THE SYSTEM HAS MISSING BIOS FILE(S) AND THE GAME MAY NOT WORK CORRECTLY.\nDO YOU WANT TO LAUNCH IT ANYWAY?"),
  520  						_("YES"), [this, game, options, center] { launch(game, options, center, false); },
  521  						_("NO"), nullptr, ICON_ERROR);
  522  
  523  					mWindow->pushGui(gui);
  524  					return false;
  525  				}
  526  			}
  527  		}
  528  	}
  529  
  530  	return true;
  531  }
  532  
  533  void ViewController::launch(FileData* game, LaunchGameOptions options, Vector3f center, bool allowCheckLaunchOptions)
  534  {
  535  	if (allowCheckLaunchOptions && !checkLaunchOptions(game, options, center))
  536  		return;
  537  
  538  	if(game->getType() != GAME)
  539  	{
  540  		LOG(LogError) << "tried to launch something that isn't a game";
  541  		return;
  542  	}
  543  
  544  	if (game->getSourceFileData()->getSystem()->hasPlatformId(PlatformIds::IMAGEVIEWER))
  545  	{
  546  		auto ext = Utils::String::toLower(Utils::FileSystem::getExtension(game->getPath()));
  547  
  548  		if (Utils::FileSystem::isVideo(game->getPath()))
  549  			GuiVideoViewer::playVideo(mWindow, game->getPath());
  550  		else if (ext == ".pdf")
  551  			GuiImageViewer::showPdf(mWindow, game->getPath());
  552  		else if (ext == ".cbz")
  553  			GuiImageViewer::showCbz(mWindow, game->getPath());
  554  		else if (!Utils::FileSystem::isAudio(game->getPath()))
  555  		{
  556  			auto gameImage = game->getImagePath();
  557  			if (Utils::FileSystem::exists(gameImage))
  558  			{
  559  				auto imgViewer = new GuiImageViewer(mWindow);
  560  
  561  				for (auto sibling : game->getParent()->getChildrenListToDisplay())
  562  					imgViewer->add(sibling->getImagePath());
  563  
  564  				imgViewer->setCursor(gameImage);
  565  				mWindow->pushGui(imgViewer);
  566  			}
  567  		}
  568  		return;
```

```text
  947  	// removed on YES as the download starts and on LATER as the player
  948  	// declines, so a crash or a power cut while the prompt is on screen
  949  	// leaves it for the next boot rather than losing the continuation.
  950  	std::string journeyMarker = "/storage/.config/.cloud-journey-pending";
  951  	bool journeyPending = Utils::FileSystem::exists(journeyMarker);
  952  	// What else the restore form had ticked (JourneyTiers, audit #307
  953  	// PL-029): the continuation is built from it, and the prompt names it.
  954  	// Read uncached, like every file another process leaves. A record with
  955  	// no marker is a settings restore that failed or never ran, and goes.
  956  	// A marker with no record is one an earlier build left, and keeps the
  957  	// continuation and the prompt it always had (D-WORKFLOW-050).
  958  	const std::string journeyRecord = JourneyTiers::PATH;
  959  	JourneyTiers::Tiers journeyTiers;
  960  	if (Utils::FileSystem::exists(journeyRecord, false))
  961  	{
  962  		if (journeyPending)
  963  			journeyTiers = JourneyTiers::parse(Utils::FileSystem::readAllText(journeyRecord));
  964  		else
  965  			Utils::FileSystem::removeFile(journeyRecord);
  966  	}
  967  	if (journeyPending && journeyTiers.known && !journeyTiers.any())
  968  	{
  969  		// Nothing else was ticked: nothing to offer. This build's form asks
  970  		// for no marker then; one left anyway is consumed here.
  971  		LOG(LogInfo) << "journey: the marker's record names nothing to restore; consumed";
  972  		std::remove(journeyMarker.c_str());
  973  		Utils::FileSystem::removeFile(journeyRecord);
  974  		journeyPending = false;
  975  	}
  976  	if (journeyPending)
  977  	{
  978  		std::string question;
  979  		if (!journeyTiers.known)
  980  			question = _("YOUR SETTINGS WERE RESTORED.\n\nDOWNLOAD YOUR GAMES, BIOS FILES, AND SAVES FROM THE CLOUD NOW?");
  981  		else if (journeyTiers.saves && journeyTiers.content && journeyTiers.media)
  982  			question = _("YOUR SETTINGS WERE RESTORED.\n\nRESTORE YOUR SAVES, ROMS, BIOS, AND GAME CONTENT FROM THE CLOUD NOW?");
  983  		else if (journeyTiers.saves && journeyTiers.content)
  984  			question = _("YOUR SETTINGS WERE RESTORED.\n\nRESTORE YOUR SAVES, ROMS, AND BIOS FROM THE CLOUD NOW?");
  985  		else if (journeyTiers.saves && journeyTiers.media)
  986  			question = _("YOUR SETTINGS WERE RESTORED.\n\nRESTORE YOUR SAVES AND GAME CONTENT FROM THE CLOUD NOW?");
```

## es-core/src/watchers/WatchersManager.cpp @ 16dbac5211: lines 157-203 (excerpt of 203)

```text
  157  }
  158  
  159  void WatchersManager::run()
  160  {
  161  	while (mRunning)
  162  	{
  163  		// ThreadLock
  164  		{
  165  			std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mThreadLock);
  166  
  167  			if (mPaused)
  168  				mEvent.wait(lock, [this]() { return !mPaused || !mRunning; });
  169  			else
  170  				mEvent.wait_for(lock, std::chrono::seconds(1));
  171  
  172  			if (!mRunning)
  173  				return;
  174  
  175  			if (mPaused)
  176  				continue;
  177  		}
  178  
  179  		// Notification Lock
  180  		{
  181  			std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mWatchersLock);
  182  
  183  			auto now = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
  184  
  185  			for (auto item : mWatchers)
  186  			{
  187  				if (!mRunning)
  188  					return;
  189  
  190  				if (!item->component->enabled())
  191  					continue;
  192  
  193  				if (now < item->nextCheckTime)
  194  					continue;
  195  
  196  				if (item->component->check())
  197  					NotifyComponentChanged(item->component);
  198  
  199  				item->nextCheckTime = now + std::chrono::milliseconds(item->component->updateTime());
  200  			}
  201  		}
  202  	}
  203  }
```

```text
  223  			// Non-blocking probe: returns true if the key's existence is already
  224  			// recorded in the cache (either exists or known-not-to-exist).
  225  			// Never calls stat64 — safe to call from the render thread.
  226  			static bool isCached(const std::string& key)
  227  			{
  228  				if (!Settings::UseFileCache())
  229  					return false;
```

```text
  253  
  254  static void offlineScanRefresh(const std::weak_ptr<MultiLineMenuEntry>& weak)
  255  {
  256  	auto entry = weak.lock();
  257  	if (!entry)
  258  		return;
  259  	const bool on = offlineScanOn();
  260  	const bool online = offlineScanOnline();
  261  	// A run in flight keeps the row lit whatever the gates say now.
  262  	entry->setDimmed((!on || !offlineScanSignedIn() || !online) && !offlineScanShowsRun());
  263  	// Only when the words change: the refresher below asks once a second,
  264  	// and a line set to itself would still lay the row out again.
  265  	const std::string text = offlineScanDetail(on, online);
  266  	if (text != entry->getDescription())
  267  		entry->setDescription(text);
  268  }
```
